*Area:* intake · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** The same parameter is governed differently depending on where it is answered. In the workspace, a parameter with require_reasoning cannot be completed without a reason. At intake, the query does not even fetch require_reasoning, the reason box is always 'optional', and task generation marks the task Completed with reason = null. Approvers then see a graded parameter with no rationale. Separately, submitCase generates Completed, graded tasks but never calls updateCycleScore. A freshly submitted case shows no live score even though the RM graded several parameters. The score appears only after some later workspace task is completed, so early triage by the KAM runs on missing information.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:292-298 - select omits require_reasoning`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:817-823 - reason textarea is always 'Reason or notes (optional)'`
- `src/utils/engine.ts:306-321 - RM draft answer becomes status 'Completed' with reason answer?.reason || null`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:317-319 - workspace enforces 'Reasoning is required for this parameter.'`
- `src/utils/engine.ts:131-219 - submitCase never calls updateCycleScore`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:349, 477 - the only updateCycleScore call sites`

**Recommendation.** Fetch require_reasoning in fetchRmIntakeTasks. Label the reason '(required)' and add it to the client-side missing check, listed in the linked error summary. Validate again in handleNewCase, or have generateStageTasks set status 'Pending' when reasoning is required but absent. Call updateCycleScore(cycle.id) at the end of submitCase so the receipt and workspace show the initial score.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. The fetchRmIntakeTasks select (292-298) omits require_reasoning. The intake textarea is always 'Reason or notes (optional)' (817-823). generateStageTasks uses select('*') but never checks require_reasoning, and it sets status Completed with reason=null (311-323). The workspace enforces the rule at [id]/actions.ts:317-319, and TaskCompleteForm labels it as required. submitCase never calls updateCycleScore. The only call sites are [id]/actions.ts:349 and 477. OverviewTab:225 therefore shows 'Not computed' right after submit, even though graded tasks exist. The recommendation is sound.

### #16 [P1] Party picker does not scale: a native select capped at 200 rows, with no search and no duplicate warnings
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm

**Problem.** Parties load once, limited to 200 sorted by name, into a plain <select>. Once the master holds more than 200 active parties, later names simply do not appear. The RM concludes the party does not exist and creates a duplicate through 'Add New'. Even under 200, scrolling a native list of legal names (code shown only for customers) is slow and error-prone when several entities share similar names. There is also no warning that the party already has an open or in-review case for this site, which the doctrine requires.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:134-143 - fetchParties .limit(200), no search parameter`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:474-479 - customer native select, legal_name + customer_code only`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:513-518 - contractor native select, no code or city shown`
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:166-173 - lastCase is fetched but open or in-flight cases are not checked`

**Recommendation.** Replace both selects with a server-searched combobox (shadcn Command/Popover) that matches legal name, display name, customer code, GSTIN, PAN and phone. Show code, city and type in each row and support keyboard selection. After selection, call a fetchOpenCasesForParty and show an attention-toned banner such as 'Party has 1 case In Review (CF-1042)' with a link. Offer 'Create new party' at the bottom of the empty result, pre-filled with the search text.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. fetchParties (actions.ts:134-143) uses .limit(200) ordered by legal_name with no search parameter. The admin fetchParties in admin/actions.ts does support ilike search and limit 1000, but intake does not use it. Customer options show legal_name and customer_code. Contractor options show legal_name and influencer_subtype only. fetchPartyDetails checks lastCase but not whether open or in-review cases already exist. Parties beyond the first 200 are silently missing, which leads to duplicate party creation. P1 is reasonable for a growing master. The combobox recommendation fits the shadcn stack.

### #17 [P1] Add-party dialog drops the credit limit, ignores which slot opened it, and can leave a selection that is set but not shown
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam

**Problem.** The dialog has several silent failures. (1) The 'Credit Limit (₹)' field is submitted as credit_limit, but upsertParty never maps it, and the real column is credit_line_amount, so whatever the RM types is discarded without notice. (2) The intake tracks whether 'Add New' was clicked for the customer or the contractor slot but never passes it in, so the dialog always defaults to 'Both'. (3) After creation, refreshParties sets the id but skips the select handler, so the context card, credit limit, outstanding exposure and reused answers never load. (4) If the RM picks type 'Customer' from the contractor slot, the contractor filter hides the new party while contractorPartyId is still set. The select looks blank but Continue is enabled. (5) Customer Code is required, but auto-generate is offered only for influencers. A customer needs a hand-typed code ('e.g. CUST-001'), there is no GSTIN or PAN format check, and nothing checks for duplicates.

**Evidence.**
- `src/components/admin/PartyDialog.tsx:176-179 - name="credit_limit", defaultValue editingParty?.credit_limit`
- `src/app/admin/actions.ts:27-39 - upsertParty payload has no credit limit field`
- `src/utils/creditLine.ts:11-14 - limit is read from parties.credit_line_amount`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:63, 468, 507, 889-893 - partyTypeForDialog is set but never passed to PartyDialog`
- `src/components/admin/PartyDialog.tsx:22, 30 - type defaults to 'both'`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:81-91 - refreshParties sets the id but does not call handleCustomerSelect or handleContractorSelect`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:515-516 - contractor filter excludes party_type 'customer'`
- `src/components/admin/PartyDialog.tsx:107-114 - code required; auto-generate only for influencer or both`

**Recommendation.** Add a defaultType prop and pass 'customer' or 'influencer' from the slot that opened the dialog, locking the type unless the RM expands options. Either map credit_limit to credit_line_amount in upsertParty (with an audit event) or remove the field for non-admins if limits are governed elsewhere. Never show an input that is silently dropped. In onSuccess, call the matching select handler so the details load. Auto-generate customer codes server-side when blank. Validate GSTIN and PAN format inline, and check for an existing PAN or GSTIN before insert, offering 'Use existing party'.

*Verification:* confirmed: All five sub-claims verified. (1) PartyDialog:176-179 posts name=credit_limit with defaultValue editingParty?.credit_limit, which is the wrong column even when editing. upsertParty (admin/actions.ts:27-39) has no limit field, while creditLine.ts reads credit_line_amount, so the value is silently dropped. (2) PartyDialogProps has no type prop and the default is 'both' (22, 30). partyTypeForDialog is only used inside refreshParties. (3) refreshParties (81-91) sets the id without calling the select handler, so no details, limit, exposure or reused answers load. (4) The contractor filter (516) excludes 'customer', but the id is still set and canGoNext(1) passes while the select shows its placeholder. (5) Customer code is required, and auto-generate is offered only for influencer/both. Note that 'both' (the default) does show auto-generate, but it produces a contractor-style ID. There is no GSTIN/PAN validation or duplicate check. A governance caution applies: an RM setting the credit limit may be deliberately disallowed, so removing the field for RMs may be the right fix rather than mapping it.

### #18 [P1] One approval closes the round, but the copy promises 'all required approvers', and an approver can vote twice
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, founder_admin, kam, rm

**Problem.** isFullyApproved is computed as 'every decision recorded so far is approve', so the first approver to click Approve moves the case straight to Approved. The confirmation tells the approver the opposite: 'If all required approvers approve…' and 'The credit days shown in this confirmation become governing terms', yet no credit days appear in the confirmation for a normal approve. No roster, required count, or 'who else must still decide' is shown anywhere. canDecide doesn't check whether the current user already decided, so after deciding they still see the form and can insert a second decision. No receipt follows submission; the page just revalidates.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:619-622 — isFullyApproved = allDecisions.every(approve), so one approval is enough`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:23-27 — consequence copy promises multi-approver semantics and 'credit days shown in this confirmation'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:340-351 — confirmation shows no terms unless the founder overrides`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:249,289 — no check for the current user's existing decision (My Work filters this at src/app/page.tsx:334-337, the workspace doesn't)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:606,704 — insert then revalidate; no receipt (Principle 13 / §14.5)`

**Recommendation.** Decide the rule and make the UI say it. If one approver is enough, rewrite the consequence copy to 'Your approval finalizes this round and moves the case to Approved', and show the exact terms being approved (days, exposure, tranches) in the confirmation. If it isn't, add required approvers or quorum to the round and show 'n of m approved · waiting on X'. Hide the form and show 'You approved on <date>' once the user has decided, and enforce one decision per approver on the server. After submitting, show a receipt banner with the new state, timestamp, actor and next owner.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: isFullyApproved = every decision so far is approve (actions.ts:620-621), so the first approve closes the round and sets the case to Approved. The consequence copy (ApprovalsTab:23-27) promises multi-approver semantics and 'credit days shown in this confirmation', but the confirmation shows no days unless the founder overrides. There is no roster or quorum, and no receipt after submitting. Refuted: 'an approver can vote twice'. Every decision path closes the round: approve because the all-approve check passes, reject and return explicitly. After revalidation isOpen is false and the form is gone. The only remaining risk is a concurrent double-submit race; approval_decisions has no unique (round, approver) constraint. Downgraded to P1: the copy misleads about process and finality, but the terms being approved are visible in the brief directly above.

### #19 [P1] Board override days get labelled 'Policy recommendation', and the recommended terms show up differently on four surfaces
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, board_member, founder_admin, rm, kam

**Problem.** Several places treat cycle.approved_credit_days as the policy recommendation unless a founder_credit_days_override audit event exists. A board 'override' writes new days into approved_credit_days without writing that event, so the score card and the Decision brief then show the board's override days as the 'Policy recommendation', and the real policy output disappears. Even without overrides, terms appear in four places with different logic: header 'Requested terms', the score-card 'Policy recommendation', the Summary ladder plus the Review Cycle card's 'Policy recommendation / Approved terms', and the Decision ladder. Approvers can't tell which number is authoritative.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:108 — recommendedDays falls back to cycle.approved_credit_days`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:291-298 — labelled 'Policy recommendation'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:69,145-146 — recommended layer uses the same fallback, 'Policy engine output — not a human decision'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/actions.ts:159-162 — board override overwrites approved_credit_days and writes no founder_credit_days_override metadata`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:224-229 — Review Cycle card repeats the recommendation and approved terms with its own labels`

**Recommendation.** Store policy_recommended_credit_days on review_cycles when scoring runs, and keep approved_credit_days strictly for human-approved terms. Make every override path (founder and board) write to an override column with actor and reason. Show terms through a single TermsLadder in one persistent location (see the decision-rail opportunity) and delete the duplicates: the score-card days and the Review Cycle card days.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed. recommendedDays falls back to cycle.approved_credit_days (CaseWorkspace:108) and is labelled 'Policy recommendation' (:291-298). The board override writes approved_credit_days with no founder_credit_days_override metadata (board/actions.ts:159-162), so after a board override the score card shows the override days as the policy recommendation. Terms do appear on 4+ surfaces with different labels. Additional evidence: updateCycleScore writes the band's days into approved_credit_days (scoring.ts:381), so the one column holds both the policy recommendation and the approved terms, and any later rescore reverts an override. This strongly supports the separate-column recommendation. Downgraded to P1: the mislabel appears after the decision, on the header score card. The Summary ladder labels it 'approved' when isApprovedPlus, and the Decision brief's recommended layer only renders while a round is open. So the mislabel corrupts the record rather than misleading a live decision.

### #20 [P1] Every server-action error wipes the workspace and shows 'Failed to load case details… might have been deleted'
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* bdo, accounts, kam, rm, ordinary_approver, board_member, founder_admin

**Problem.** Server actions report validation and authorization problems by throwing: 'Reasoning is required', 'A delay reason is required', 'Only KAM or Admin can record a negotiation outcome', 'Founder override must be higher than…'. None of the forms catch these errors, so they reach the route error boundary, which replaces the whole case with a message suggesting the case was deleted. The reviewer loses their typed reasoning and grade, any optimistic task update rolls back, and the message gives them no idea what to fix.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:314-319 — throws on missing delay reason or reasoning`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:824-826 — throws for RM on 'Record accepted terms'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:45-48 — startTransition(await handleCompleteTask) with no try/catch or inline error`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/error.tsx:22-25 — 'Failed to load case details… might have been deleted'`
- `src/components/creditflow/ConfirmDialog.tsx:81-84 — awaits the action with no error handling`

**Recommendation.** Make mutations return a typed result ({ok:false, field?, message}) instead of throwing, and use useActionState so the message appears inline next to the field while the input is kept. Keep error.tsx for real load failures and reword it. Add an inline role-mismatch message for authorization failures (see the header actions finding).

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. Actions throw, the forms and ConfirmDialog (ConfirmDialog:81-84) have no try/catch, and TaskCompleteForm:45-48 awaits inside startTransition, so errors reach the route error.tsx. Its copy (lines 22-25) suggests the case was deleted. Mitigation the finding missed: the delayReason select and reasonNote have HTML `required`, so the most common cases are blocked on the client. Real triggers remain: (a) editing a Completed task past its SLA always throws. isTaskOverdue returns false for Completed tasks, so the delay select is hidden, but the server's isOverdue check (actions.ts:313-316) ignores status. (b) Whitespace-only reasoning. (c) A require_reasoning parameter on a non-scoring task, which has no reason input. (d) RM using 'Record accepted terms'. (e) A non-admin finalizing a board decision. (f) Persona or policy mismatch. The recommendation is sound.

### #21 [P1] Header actions ignore role, and their forms only open inside the Summary tab
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam, ordinary_approver, board_member, bdo, accounts

**Problem.** The command bar shows 'Record accepted terms', 'Unlock / reopen', 'Change personas', 'Withdraw' and 'Appeal decision' based on case status only. Board members, approvers, BDO and Accounts see Withdraw and Change personas. KAM and RM see Unlock, which the server rejects for them. Approved lists the RM as owner with the next action 'record accepted terms', but handleCounterOffer allows only KAM or Admin, so the named owner can't do the one thing they're told to do. And because the counter-offer, unlock and persona forms render inside OverviewTab, clicking these buttons from the Decision or Work tab (Awaiting Approval defaults to Decision) does nothing visible. 'Unlock / reopen' also shows when there is no active cycle, but its form needs cycle.is_active, which leaves a dead button.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:195-221 — buttons gated only by status, not role`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:336-350 — toggled forms live only in the Summary TabsContent`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:119,143 — unlock and counter-offer forms also need cycle.is_active`
- `src/lib/vocabulary.ts:78-84 — Approved ownerRole 'rm'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:823-826 — counter-offer limited to kam/founder_admin`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:801-803 — unlock limited to approver/board/founder_admin`

**Recommendation.** Build one permission map, e.g. a canDo(role, status, action) helper in src/lib that both the UI and the actions use. Show only the actions the current role can take, and put the rest in an overflow menu with a 'Requires KAM' hint. Open these flows in a Dialog or Sheet from the header rather than inside a tab. Align the Approved owner with whoever can actually record acceptance, either RM in the action or KAM in the vocabulary.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. The header buttons (CaseWorkspace:195-221) are gated only by status. Withdraw shows to all roles, but the server allows rm/kam/founder only. Unlock shows to KAM/RM, but the server allows approver/board/founder only (actions.ts:801-803). Approved's ownerRole is 'rm' (vocabulary.ts), but handleCounterOffer is KAM or Admin only (actions.ts:824-826). The Unlock, counter-offer and persona forms render only in the Summary TabsContent, and Radix unmounts inactive content (no forceMount anywhere). From Decision, the default tab in Awaiting Approval, clicking them does nothing visible until the user later switches to Summary. Unlock also shows when cycle is null (for example in Draft), while its form needs cycle.is_active, so the button is dead. The header 'Appeal decision' also has no role gate. The recommendation is sound.

### #22 [P1] 'Selective Unlock' does nothing except write an audit line
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, founder_admin, rm

**Problem.** The unlock form says 'Unlocking a section allows editing', and the button reads 'Unlock Section'. handleSelectiveUnlock only logs an audit event and revalidates. No field is unlocked, no re-review is flagged, the RM isn't notified, and the form closes without feedback. The user thinks they've reopened the commercial section, the RM can't edit anything, and the audit trail records an unlock that never happened.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:122-137 — copy promises editing, onSubmit closes the card with no receipt`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:797-817 — handleSelectiveUnlock only calls logAuditEvent`

**Recommendation.** Either build the feature (persist unlocked_sections with an expiry on the case, let the intake form read it, flag material change for re-review, notify the RM, show an 'Unlocked: Commercial · by X · reason' banner) or remove the button until it exists. Rename it to describe the actual effect, e.g. 'Return section to RM for edit'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. handleSelectiveUnlock (actions.ts:797-817) only logs an audit event and revalidates. The copy at OverviewTab:125 promises editing. No field, flag or notification is set anywhere, and onSubmit just closes the card. The audit trail records an unlock that never takes effect. Either recommendation option is sound.

### #23 [P1] The negotiation form can't record a decline cleanly, closes the case with no confirmation, and has no tranche editing
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, rm

**Problem.** In 'Counter-Offer / Negotiate Terms', 'New Composite Days' is required, so clicking 'Customer Declined' is blocked by browser validation until a number is entered. Once one is entered, that click closes the case permanently ('Closed — Customer Declined') with no confirmation and no reason captured. The helper text says 'You may restructure tranches', but there are no tranche inputs, and the accepted record just copies proposed_tranches. The ≤ approved-days limit exists only as an HTML max attribute; the server doesn't check it. Approval validity and expiry are shown elsewhere but not in this form, and the form never compares requested, approved and proposed values side by side.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:150 — copy promises tranche restructuring`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:153 — compositeDays required, which blocks the Declined submit`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:159 — 'Customer Declined' submits immediately, no confirmation or reason`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:844-856 — no server check that compositeDays ≤ approved_credit_days; tranches copied unchanged`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:866-870 — 'dropped' closes the case with no reason field`

**Recommendation.** Split this into two explicit outcomes. (1) 'Record acceptance': a dialog with accepted days and editable tranches pre-filled from the approved structure, a live check against the approved maximum and validity, enforced on the server, and an option to trigger re-review when the change is material. (2) 'Customer declined / dropped': a ConfirmDialog that requires a reason code, explains the case will close, and suggests archiving. Show an inline mini terms ladder (requested → approved → proposed) in the dialog.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. compositeDays is `required` (OverviewTab:153), and the 'Customer Declined' button has no formNoValidate, so browser validation blocks a decline until a number is entered. Once entered, 'dropped' closes the case (actions.ts:866-870) with no confirmation and no reason. The helper copy promises tranche restructuring, but there are no tranche inputs, and accepted copies proposed_tranches unchanged (:852). The server checks only for NaN or negative values, with no ≤ approved_credit_days check (:844-847). The recommendation is sound.

### #24 [P1] The Work tab can't waive a task, mark it waiting, or force-ready a stage, even though server actions for two of these exist
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, founder_admin

**Problem.** The lifecycle is Pending → Completed/Waived plus an is_waiting flag, and StagesTab reads is_waived and is_waiting. But there is no UI to waive a required task, put one into waiting (which pauses its SLA), or force-ready a stage. handleToggleWaiting and handleForceReadyStage exist in actions.ts but no component calls them. When a required document can't be obtained, the KAM can't submit the stage ('Submit Stage' appears only when the stage is complete) and has no supported way around it. The ambiguity path that force-ready is meant to trigger can't be reached either.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/StagesTab.tsx:46-50,133 — Submit appears only when all required tasks are Completed or waived`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/StagesTab.tsx:204-209 — waiting is displayed but can't be set`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:357-407 — handleForceReadyStage is not called from any TSX`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:409-435 — handleToggleWaiting is not called from any TSX (it is also case-level, not task-level)`

**Recommendation.** Add a task overflow menu for KAM/Admin with 'Mark waiting…' (reason, expected date) and 'Waive…' (reason, required for required tasks, audited). When required tasks are still open, replace the hidden Submit button with a disabled 'Submit Stage' that lists the blockers, plus a secondary 'Force-ready with missing items…' ConfirmDialog that warns the case will go to board review.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. A grep shows handleToggleWaiting and handleForceReadyStage are referenced only in actions.ts and actions.test.ts, never from TSX. Nothing in src writes is_waived or is_waiting=true except engine.setWaiting, which is case-level. Submit renders only when stageComplete (StagesTab:133). When required tasks are open there is no CTA or blocker list, so a stuck required document leaves Withdraw as the only way forward. One correction: ambiguity is also reachable through checkAmbiguity in updateCycleScore, so it's not fully unreachable. The recommendation is sound and matches the Pending→Completed/Waived + is_waiting convention.

### #25 [P1] Board portal: non-founders see Finalize, the outcome defaults to 'Uphold', the confirmation promises a rationale field that doesn't exist, and vote revision rules contradict each other
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* board_member, founder_admin

**Problem.** Once half the roster has voted, the 'Finalize Board Outcome' card shows for every role, and board members only learn it's founder-only when the server throws. The outcome select defaults to 'Uphold Existing Terms (Approve normally)'. For an appeal of a rejection, 'existing terms' is undefined, and two clicks approve the case. The confirmation says 'The decision, tally and your rationale are permanently audited', but there is no rationale field unless 'override' is chosen. Choosing an outcome that contradicts the tally needs no justification, despite the copy saying it must be justified. Vote revision: the portal allows it ('you may change it until then') and the server upserts, while the workspace BoardSection says 'Contact the founder if you need to revise it' and hides the form. Rationale is required in the portal but optional in the workspace. Quorum isn't shown before voting, and the decision packet only opens in a new tab.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/BoardClient.tsx:229 — Finalize card gated only by vote count, not role`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/BoardClient.tsx:254-256 — 'uphold' is the default option`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/BoardClient.tsx:258-263,289-295 — copy promises a justification and an audited rationale, but no field exists`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/actions.ts:126-128 — admin-only enforced on the server`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/actions.ts:163-167 — uphold doesn't stamp finalized_at or validity_expires_at (unlike actions.ts:640-645)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:520,528-531 vs BoardClient.tsx:194,209 — contradictory revise and rationale rules`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/BoardClient.tsx:102-106 — packet only reachable in a new tab`

**Recommendation.** Show Finalize only to founder_admin. Leave the outcome unselected by default, with labels that state the effect ('Approve at X days', 'Reject', 'Override to N days'). Require a finalization rationale every time, plus an explicit override reason when the outcome conflicts with the tally. Stamp finalized_at and validity on uphold and override. Pick one revise policy and one rationale rule and use the same component on both surfaces. Show quorum and the decision rule above the ballot, and embed a read-only decision brief (terms ladder, drivers, exposure) in the portal.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed. The Finalize card is gated only by totalVotesCast >= roster/2 (BoardClient:229), which is also true with an empty roster (0>=0). 'uphold' is the default option (:254). The confirmation promises 'your rationale' (:293), but there is no rationale field unless override is chosen. Choosing reject against an approve majority needs no justification. Neither uphold nor override stamps finalized_at or validity_expires_at (board/actions.ts:159-167, compared with actions.ts:640-645). The revise policy contradicts itself: the portal says 'you may change it' and upserts, while the workspace says 'Contact the founder' and hides the form. Rationale is required in the portal but optional in the workspace. The packet is reachable only in a new tab. Overstated: board members are told up front in the card copy ('Authority boundary: only the founder finalizes'), so they don't learn it only from a server throw. The decision rule ('majority of votes cast…') is shown in the Participation card; only an explicit quorum threshold is missing. The recommendation is sound.

### #26 [P1] Cases without a decided amount or tranche schedule show '0d' overdue in Collections and refuse payment entry
*Area:* money · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* accounts, kam

**Problem.** The server includes a case in the overdue list using a fallback (billing date + composite credit days) when decided_bill_amount or tranches are missing. The client then computes no overdue tranches for it. The row shows a '0d' pill, lands in the 1–30 band, has no tranche table and no 'Settle' shortcut. Pressing 'Save payment' errors with 'No overdue tranches found for this case.' This happens to exactly the cases where the KAM has not yet entered the decided amount (the Ledger lets the RM start billing first) and to legacy imports. Those are often the oldest and riskiest debts, and they are buried at the bottom and cannot be booked.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/page.tsx:57-83 — fallback DPD from composite_credit_days when tranches/decided missing`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:108 — computeOverdueTranches returns [] without decided_bill_amount`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:294 — worstDpd = max(0, ...[]) = 0`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1105-1112 — submitPayment blocks when no payTranche`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:310 — decided amount is a separate, later KAM step`

**Recommendation.** Use the shared ledger utility (see the outstanding-base finding) on both server and client, with an explicit fallback single tranche 'Full amount due {billing_date + credit days}'. Allow payment entry without a tranche (the waterfall allocates anyway). Show a warning chip 'Decided amount missing — DPD estimated from credit terms' with a link to the Ledger so the gap is fixed at source.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. page.tsx:81-82 falls back to billing_date + composite_credit_days when tranches or decided are missing, so these cases enter the overdue list. Client computeOverdueTranches (108) returns [] without decided_bill_amount, so worstDpd = max(0) = 0 (294), the bucket is 1-30, there is no tranche table and no Settle button (1385). submitPayment (1108-1111) blocks with 'No overdue tranches found for this case.' handleSaveBillingDetails (138-200) sets Billing Active without a decided amount, and the decided amount is a separate KAM form (LedgerTab 310). These cases are also excluded from upcoming (page.tsx:94). Payment can still be logged on the Ledger by KAM/admin, so P1 is right. Recommendation is sound.

### #27 [P1] Payment entry has no guardrails against typos, overpayment, future dates or duplicates
*Area:* money · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* accounts, kam

**Problem.** Both payment forms accept any positive integer through a raw type=number input. There is no Indian-grouped echo, so ₹1500000 vs ₹150000 is easy to mistype. There is no warning when the amount exceeds outstanding (which silently auto-closes the case), no max date (future-dated payments are accepted), and no duplicate check for the same amount and date. On the Ledger the default date is UTC, so before 05:30 IST it pre-fills yesterday. In Collections the inputs rely on placeholders with no labels, and reference/UTR is optional free text. Accounts is the most frequent user of these forms, and every typo here becomes an audit-logged edit later.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1352-1373 — unlabeled number/date/note inputs, no min/max`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:135 — payDate from new Date().toISOString() (UTC)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:470-478 — no max date, no amount echo`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:264-265 — only checks amount > 0 and date present`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:325-327 — overpayment silently auto-closes`

**Recommendation.** Build one shared <PaymentEntry> used by both screens. Show a live echo ('₹15,00,000 · fifteen lakh') and set max=today in IST. Add an inline warning when amount > outstanding ('This exceeds outstanding by ₹X and will close the case — confirm'), a soft duplicate warning when the same amount and date already exist, and a required UTR/reference for Accounts. Mirror the validations server-side.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. In Collections (1352-1373) the amount, date and note inputs are raw with placeholders, no labels and no min/max. There is no reference/UTR field, only a free-text note. LedgerTab:135 uses new Date().toISOString(), which is UTC, so it pre-fills yesterday before 05:30 IST. Collections correctly uses istToday() (1074). The Ledger amount has min=1 but no max date and no echo. The server checks only amount>0 and that a date is present (264-265), and parseInt silently truncates decimals. Overpayment closes the case through checkAndCloseCase (325-327) with no warning. One small correction: on the Ledger these forms are KAM-only (see finding 0), so 'Accounts is the most frequent user' applies only to Collections today. Recommendation is sound.

### #28 [P1] 'Mark as Settled / Close' silently becomes a write-off request, and write-off and credit-note approvals have no consequence preview
*Area:* money · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, founder_admin

**Problem.** The KAM clicks 'Mark as Settled / Close' and gets a native confirm saying only 'Collected amount will be evaluated'. If the shortfall exceeds WRITE_OFF_SLIPPAGE_PERCENTAGE, the case moves to Pending Write-Off Approval, or it closes with a shortfall under the threshold, and the KAM is never shown the shortfall, the threshold or which path will happen. The admin's 'Approve Write-Off' is a green success-styled button with no confirmation, no note field (the action accepts 'note' but the UI never sends one), and no pending state. Credit-note Approve/Reject likewise show no 'Promised ₹9.5L → ₹9.0L' preview. These are the most irreversible money actions in the product.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:599-611 — close button + vague confirm()`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:503-524 — branches to write-off request or shortfall close`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:327-352 — write-off approve/reject forms, no note, no confirm, approve styled bg-success`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:642 — note param accepted but never supplied`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:695-714 — credit-note approve/reject with no impact preview`

**Recommendation.** Rename the button by computed outcome: 'Close — fully collected', 'Close with ₹12,000 shortfall (within 10% tolerance)' or 'Request write-off of ₹1.2L (18%)'. Open a dialog that shows shortfall, threshold, next owner and a required reason. For write-off approval require a note, show shortfall %, customer/party history and the payment log summary, and use neutral/destructive styling rather than success green. For credit notes show the before/after promised amount and the effect on outstanding and tranche dues.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. LedgerTab 599-611 shows a 'Mark as Settled / Close' button with only a vague native confirm(). handleAttemptClose (499-524) silently branches to a full close, a close with shortfall under the threshold, or Pending Write-Off Approval, and the result is never shown. Write-off approve/reject (327-352) are plain form actions with no confirm, no note input and no pending state, and Approve is styled bg-success. handleWriteOffApproval reads 'note' (642), but the UI never sends it. Credit-note approve/reject (695-714) have no before/after preview. The pending write-off banner does show the shortfall in rupees, but not the % or threshold. Recommendation is sound and uses semantic tokens.

### #29 [P1] 'Remind Accounts' shows success but the reminder is never stored or delivered
*Area:* money · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, accounts

**Problem.** The RM clicks 'Remind Accounts' and immediately sees alert('Reminder sent to Accounts team.'), fired before the action even resolves. The action inserts into escalation_logs with escalation_id null and a case_id field. That table has escalation_id NOT NULL and no case_id column, so the insert fails and the error is swallowed. No notification is created. The RM believes Accounts was nudged, Accounts never hears about it, and the bill stays unattached.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:378-385 — alert() runs synchronously after wrap()`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:834-840 — insert {case_id, escalation_id: null} with result ignored`
- `supabase/migrations/20260514000000_remote_schema.sql:395-403 — escalation_logs: escalation_id NOT NULL, no case_id column`

**Recommendation.** Create a real notification to users with the accounts role (the notifications table) plus an audit event, and surface the result: 'Reminder sent to Accounts (3 people) · 14:05'. Disable the button for 24h showing 'Reminded today by you'. Check the insert error and show a failure state.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. LedgerTab 379-383 calls wrap(), which runs startTransition without awaiting, then alert() fires immediately. remindAccounts (834-840) inserts {case_id, escalation_id: null} into escalation_logs and ignores the result. The schema (remote_schema.sql 395-403) has escalation_id NOT NULL and no case_id column, and no later migration changes escalation_logs (only remote_schema mentions it). The insert therefore always fails silently and nothing is notified. A notifications table exists (remote_schema 527). Recommendation is sound.

### #30 [P1] Only KAM/Admin can log contact; RM and Accounts can move cases to 'Done today' without leaving a trace, and the shared board hides who moved what
*Area:* money · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, accounts, kam

**Problem.** The RM owns the customer relationship and often makes the call, but sees only 'View log' and cannot record a call, visit or PTP. Accounts also cannot note a remittance conversation. Yet setBoardStatus lets RM and Accounts move a card to 'Done today', which is global per case, so a KAM sees cases marked done with no record of what was done. The board stores 'by' but never shows it, contrary to §12.10. 'Untouched 14d+' counts only HQ logs. A case that received a payment yesterday, or was escalated with a note, is still flagged 'No contact logged'.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:216 — canHqChat = kam | founder_admin`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1410-1412 — RM sees 'View log' only`
- `src/app/collections/actions.ts:142-144 — server rejects non-KAM logs`
- `src/app/collections/actions.ts:210,227 — rm/accounts may set board status; 'by' stored`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:432-437,1014-1020 — board never displays mover`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:283-286,392 — last contact from hqLogs only`

**Recommendation.** Let rm (own cases) and accounts log contact and PTPs. Moving to 'Done today' should open a one-line 'What happened?' prompt that creates the log entry. Show 'Moved by Ravi · 11:20' on board cards, or make the board personal per user as §12.10 suggests. Count payments and escalation notes as contact activity, labelled 'Last activity' rather than 'Last contact'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. canHqChat is kam|founder_admin (216). RM and Accounts see 'View log' (1411), and addHqCollectionLog rejects non-KAM roles (142-144). setBoardStatus (210, 227) allows rm/accounts and stores 'by', but board cards (1014-1020) show rmName and never the mover. lastContact comes only from hqLogs (283-286), so payments and escalation notes (escalation_logs) don't count, and 'No contact logged' (1193) can be wrong. One mitigation: RM can escalate with an optional reason, written to escalation_logs, which leaves some trace, but it is not shown as contact. P1 is reasonable because RM cannot record PTPs, which are central to collections.

### #31 [P1] Saving a parameter silently rewrites values the admin never touched: weight 0 becomes 1 and persistence_scope is wiped
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm

**Problem.** upsertParameter uses `parseFloat(weight) || 1.0`. A weight of 0, meaning 'exclude this parameter', is saved as 1, so excluded evidence starts counting fully. The engine and the weight-matrix action both deliberately treat 0 as valid, which makes this inconsistency invisible. The parameter dialog also has no persistence_scope field, and the action writes `persistence_scope || 'none'`. Opening any party-persistent parameter and pressing Save turns off party-answer reuse at case intake, with no warning. sla_days = 0 is likewise saved as null. This is a configured behavior with no control, plus a dialog that destroys it.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:333 — `weight: parseFloat(...) || 1.0` turns 0 into 1`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:857-858 — upsertWeightMatrix keeps 0 ('0 is a legitimate weight')`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:67-68 — the engine uses ?? so that 0 means excluded`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:342 — `persistence_scope: formData.get('persistence_scope') || 'none'``
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:241-427 — the dialog has no persistence_scope input`
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:93-99 — consumer: party persistence only when persistence_scope === 'party'`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:338 — `parseInt(sla_days) || null` turns 0 into null`

**Recommendation.** Parse numbers with Number.isNaN fallbacks instead of ||. Add a visible 'Remember answer for this party across cases' toggle bound to persistence_scope, and show an 'R' flag in the table. Only write the fields the form actually rendered, either with a partial update or by passing through hidden fields with the existing values. Add a unit test that round-trips a parameter with weight 0 and persistence_scope 'party'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: actions.ts:333 uses `parseFloat(weight) || 1.0`, :338 uses `parseInt(sla_days) || null`, and :342 writes `persistence_scope || 'none'`. The dialog has no persistence_scope field. It is worse than reported: the client also shows weight 0 as 1 (ParametersClient.tsx:294 `defaultValue={editing?.weight || 1}`). Severity lowered: grep shows no UI anywhere sets persistence_scope to 'party' (only the DB default 'none'), so the wipe only hits values seeded directly in the DB. A default parameter weight of 0 is also an edge case, since exclusion is usually done per persona in the weight matrix, which handles 0 correctly. This is real silent config corruption but uncommon, so P1.

### #32 [P1] Edit on Dominance, Routing, Validity and Stage Totals does not load the row's values, so saving overwrites it with stale defaults
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** These four pages use an unkeyed side form with uncontrolled inputs (defaultValue). Clicking the Edit icon changes the card title to 'Edit …' and sets the id, but React ignores a changed defaultValue on inputs that are already mounted. The fields keep showing whatever was there before: blank, or the 0.5/0.5 and 90-day defaults. An admin who changes one field and saves writes those stale values over the real row. For example, a 0.7/0.3 dominance blend becomes 0.5/0.5, which rescoring every case that uses it. On the live policy this happens with no warning.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:46,49,65,69,75 — unkeyed form with defaultValue inputs; :109 sets editingCat`
- `src/app/policy/routing/RoutingClient.tsx:63,71,79,90,99 — same pattern; :143 setEditingRule`
- `src/app/policy/validity/ValidityClient.tsx:57,61,68,76 — same; :111`
- `src/app/policy/stages/StagesClient.tsx:40,44,53 — same; :91`

**Recommendation.** Add `key={editing?.id ?? 'new'}` to each <form>, or switch to controlled inputs. Better still, move to the shared Dialog editing pattern that Parameters, Bands and Personas already use, so every page has one CRUD grammar. Show the row being edited, for example by highlighting it in the table.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed for all four pages: unkeyed forms with defaultValue inputs and selects. I reproduced it with React 19.2.4 and jsdom. After a re-render with new defaultValue, the input's value attribute updated but the displayed .value stayed at the old default, because React's initial mount sets .value and marks the field dirty. Selects also kept their old value. Effects: Routing saves blank exposure/score, 'Any scenario' and Stage 1, which turns the rule into one that matches every case. Validity drops its band/scenario condition and resets to 90 days. Dominance resets to 0.5/0.5 (method is controlled, so it does update). Stages resets max_total to 100. Severity lowered to P1 because the stale values are visible in the form before saving (e.g. a blank required Name in Dominance). It is a broken affordance that overwrites data, not a silent one.

### #33 [P1] Stage max total is one denominator shared by customer and contractor, and duplicate rows silently fall back to 100
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, ordinary_approver, board_member

**Problem.** The engine normalizes each subject's weighted sum by a single stage_max_totals row per stage, ignoring subject type and persona. If customer and contractor parameters have different total weight, one subject can never reach 100 while the other gets capped, and that skews dominance and band outcomes. The Stages page's 'parameter capacity' check adds customer and contractor parameters together and ignores persona overrides, so its drift number is wrong for both subjects. Its warning always says 'scores will cap', even when capacity is below the configured total, which actually means scores can never reach the top band. The 'New Total' form can insert a second row for the same stage with no uniqueness check. The engine's .maybeSingle() then errors and quietly uses 100.

**Evidence.**
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:110-121 — one max_total per stage, `|| 100` fallback, Math.min cap`
- `src/app/policy/stages/page.tsx:14-19 — impliedTotals adds all parameters regardless of subject_type or persona`
- `src/app/policy/stages/StagesClient.tsx:86 — the 'scores will cap' copy is used for both directions of drift`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:802-809 — insert with no check for an existing stage`
- `src/app/policy/stages/StagesClient.tsx:42-49 — free stage picker on 'New Total'`

**Recommendation.** Work out the denominator from the model (Σ weight × top grade per subject, persona and stage) and show it as read-only. If a manual override has to stay, store it per subject and require a reason. Enforce one row per (version, stage, subject). Replace the free 'New Total' form with a fixed 3-row table. Phrase drift both ways: 'Max reachable 82/100: no case can score above Band B' versus 'Capped: weighted sum can exceed total by 18%'.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: one stage_max_totals row per stage serves as the denominator for both subjects (scoring.ts:110-121, `|| 100`, Math.min cap). impliedTotals (stages/page.tsx:14-19) adds all parameters regardless of subject_type or persona. The drift copy uses Math.abs and always says 'scores will cap', even for under-capacity. Refuted: duplicate rows cannot exist, because the schema has UNIQUE(policy_version_id, stage) (remote_schema.sql:1132), so the 'maybeSingle errors and falls back to 100' path cannot happen. What actually happens: a second 'New Total' for an existing stage fails at the DB, the error is ignored, and the audit log still records 'Stage N max total set to X'. That is a silent failure plus a false audit entry. A miscalibrated normalization is a real design flaw but does not directly lose data, so P1.

### #34 [P1] Score bands have no coverage or overlap validation; decimal final scores can land in gaps and get no credit days
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** Bands are free min/max pairs. The only check is min > max. The engine rounds the final case score to 2 decimals after dominance blending, then matches bands inclusively. With the usual integer bands (0–59, 60–100), a blended score of 59.5 matches nothing. approved_credit_days and score_band_name are then stored as null, and validity rules keyed on the band never fire. Overlaps are resolved silently by whichever band has the higher min_score. The visual map shows each band's credit days, but not its score coverage, so gaps and overlaps are invisible.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:489-491 — the only validation is min > max`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:206 — finalScore rounded to 2 decimals`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:237-247 — inclusive match, returns null on a gap`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:381-382 — null credit days and band stored on the cycle`
- `src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:57-77 — bar width shows credit days, not score coverage`

**Recommendation.** Model bands as ordered cut points on a single 0–100 axis, editing thresholds instead of min/max pairs. That makes them contiguous by construction, using half-open intervals [min, next_min). Draw the axis with any gaps or overlaps highlighted, and block publish while coverage is incomplete. Until then, add server checks for overlap and full 0–100 coverage.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: the only validation is min > max (actions.ts:489). The final score is rounded to 2 decimals (scoring.ts:206), matching is inclusive and returns null on a gap (237-247), and null days and band are written to the cycle (381-382). Cumulative subject scores are rounded to integers, but the 0.5/0.5 scenario default, weighted blends and power-law blends all produce decimals (e.g. 89.5 against test bands 70-89 and 90-100). The band inputs (BandsClient.tsx:131-136) have no step attribute, so the browser's default step=1 blocks decimal bounds like 59.99 and gaps cannot be avoided in the UI. The visual map width uses credit days. Severity lowered: gaps only affect boundary half-scores and continuous power-law results, and the outcome is a missing recommendation, not a wrong one, so P1.

### #35 [P1] Simulation is a separate simplified formula, not the engine, and has a dead Exposure input
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The only 'impact before publishing' tool takes typed-in subject scores and re-implements dominance in the browser. It skips parameters, grades, personas, normalization, persona minimum-score gates, critical-evidence ambiguity, routing and validity. Its power-law math differs from the engine: Simulation uses Math.max(contractor,1) with no clamp, while the engine clamps to 0–100, so the same inputs give different results. `exposure` is in state but never rendered or used. The runSimulation server action is a stub that returns {simulated:true}. scoring.ts says live behavior 'must match what the simulation preview shows', but it does not. The result also labels band days as 'Approved Credit Days' in success green, which presents a recommendation as an approval.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:16 — exposure state is never used`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:18-47 — local formula`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:27 vs src/utils/scoring.ts:179-186 — different power-law handling`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/actions.ts:5-8 — stub action that imports engine functions but never calls them`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:354-355 — comment claims parity with simulation`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:102-103 — 'Approved Credit Days' in text-success`
- `src/app/policy/page.tsx:38 — hub promises 'Preview scoring and ambiguity behavior'`

**Recommendation.** Split scoring.ts into pure functions (weightedSum, normalize, combine, mapBand, ambiguityReasons) that both the engine and Simulation call. Change the simulator inputs to a grade per parameter, prefilled from top or median grades, plus customer and contractor personas, dominance, scenario and exposure. Output the full trace: subject scores, normalization, final score, band, recommended days, required stage with routing reason, validity window and ambiguity reasons. Show the draft result next to the live result. Delete the stub action and the dead state.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: the exposure state is unused (SimulationClient.tsx:16). The local formula skips parameters, personas, gates and routing. Power law uses Math.max(contractor,1) with no clamp and no exponent>0 guard, while the engine has both. runSimulation is a stub that returns {simulated:true}. The scoring.ts:354-355 parity comment is false. Line 103 shows 'Approved Credit Days' in text-success. One more divergence: with no dominance selected the simulator uses the customer score, while the engine uses scenarioDefaultScore (50/50 for mixed cases). An unmatched band shows '0 days' where the engine stores null.

### #36 [P1] Publish is an unvalidated status flip: no completeness check, no diff, no change note, not atomic, and the label still says 'Draft'
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The publish dialog compares only four row counts ('Active parameters: 12 → 12'), so a draft where every weight and band changed looks identical. Nothing checks band coverage, stage totals, dominance coverage per scenario, or empty grade scales before going live. No change note is required (reasonLabel=''). publishDraftPolicy archives the current live version and then activates the new one in two unchecked writes, with no error handling and no check that versionId is a draft. A failure in between leaves no active policy, which blocks case intake. Draft labels are generated as 'vN.0 Draft' and never renamed. The live banner then reads 'Editing v3.0 Draft — the published policy'. The audit entry records the version UUID instead of its label. Multiple parallel drafts can exist, each cloned from the old live version, so publishing one quietly makes the other stale.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/PolicyVersionActions.tsx:13,29-32 — count-only comparison, reasonLabel=''`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:200-222 — two unchecked updates, no draft check, audit uses versionId`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:98 — label `v${n}.0 Draft` never updated on publish`
- `src/app/policy/PolicyContextBar.tsx:32 — shows the version_label as the published policy`
- `src/app/policy/page.tsx:65-69 — New Draft takes no label or purpose input`

**Recommendation.** Replace the dialog with a Publish Review page that has four parts. (1) A blocking validation checklist: band coverage, stage-total drift, dominance per scenario, at least one grade, no orphaned validity rules. (2) A field-level diff against live, for example 'Band B min 60→55', 'Weight: Bank statement 2→3 (Stage 1)'. (3) A back-test summary (see the opportunity finding). (4) A required change note stored in the audit log. Do the flip as a single Postgres RPC transaction. Strip 'Draft' from the label, or ask for a name, on publish. Warn when a draft's base version is no longer live.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: the publish dialog compares only 4 counts and has reasonLabel=''. publishDraftPolicy (actions.ts:200-222) runs two unchecked updates with no draft check and no error handling, and the audit entry uses the versionId UUID. The default label is `v${n}.0 Draft` and is never renamed. The '+ New Draft' form on page.tsx has no label input. Nothing prevents several drafts existing at once, each cloned from live at creation time. The recommendation is sound.

### #37 [P1] Parameters and Grades tables do not refresh after save, the dialog closes before the result, and server errors are swallowed
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** ParametersClient and GradesClient copy their props into useState once and never update it (setParams and setGrades are unused). After revalidatePath, the server sends fresh data but the table keeps showing the old list. A new parameter does not appear, an edit looks reverted, and an archived (deleted) row stays visible until a hard refresh. The dialog closes on submit before the action finishes, with no pending state or success receipt. On the server, every upsert ignores Supabase's {error} and still writes a 'saved' audit event. A failed write therefore looks like success, and the audit log is wrong. Admins will re-create items, producing duplicate parameters.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:39 — useState(initialParams); setParams never called`
- `src/app/policy/grades/GradesClient.tsx:24 — same pattern`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:241 — onSubmit={() => setOpen(false)}`
- `src/app/policy/grades/GradesClient.tsx:108 — same`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:372,383,385 — update/insert results ignored; audit logged regardless`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:437,448,496,507,561,572,616,627 — same unchecked writes for grades, bands, personas, dominance`

**Recommendation.** Render straight from props, as Bands and Personas already do. Use useActionState with SubmitButton pending states, and close the dialog only after success. Show a receipt such as 'Saved "Bank statement" to v4 draft'. Check every Supabase {error}, throw a typed message, and show it inline in the dialog. Log audit events only after a confirmed write.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: ParametersClient.tsx:39 and GradesClient.tsx:24 copy props into useState and never call setParams or setGrades. The page renders the client at the same position with no key, so after revalidatePath the RSC refresh keeps the stale state. onSubmit={() => setOpen(false)} closes the dialog before the action resolves (the action still runs). All upserts ignore the Supabase {error} and then log the audit event. Bands and Personas render from props, as the finding says.

### #38 [P1] How personas and dominance get applied to a case, and the built-in fallbacks, are hidden from the policy admin
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm

**Problem.** Personas carry minimum-score gates and weight overrides, but nothing in /policy explains when a persona applies. In fact it is picked by hand per case in the Case Overview, and the default is 'no persona'. The Personas subtitle says 'templates by entity type', but no entity-type field exists. When a case has no dominance category, the engine uses scenario defaults: customer-only, contractor-only, or a fixed 50/50 for mixed scenarios. That logic is hard-coded and never shown on the Dominance page, so an admin who sets a 70/30 category thinks it governs cases it never touches. Routing quietly defaults to Stage 1 when no rule matches. All of this is behavior with no control, and it only surfaces as unexplained scores.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:77-101 — persona and dominance chosen per case, defaulting to 'Default (no persona)' / 'Scenario default'`
- `src/app/policy/personas/PersonasClient.tsx:47 — 'by entity type' copy`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:198-214 — hard-coded scenarioDefaultScore (50/50 for mixed)`
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:35 — page says nothing about defaults or assignment`
- `src/utils/routing.ts:34 — implicit Stage 1 default`

**Recommendation.** Add an 'Applies when' rule to personas and dominance categories, for example 'scenario = contractor pays → Dominance: Contractor-heavy', and auto-assign at intake, with the per-case override kept as an audited exception. Until that exists, add a read-only 'Built-in default' row at the top of the Dominance and Routing tables showing the fallback math and stage. Change the Personas copy to say who assigns a persona, and where.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: personas and dominance are picked per case in OverviewTab.tsx:77-101 with 'Default (no persona)' and 'Scenario default'. Intake never assigns them (cases/new has no persona or dominance assignment). The PersonasClient.tsx:47 'by entity type' copy has no backing field. scenarioDefaultScore hard-codes 50/50 for mixed scenarios and is never surfaced on the Dominance page. evaluateRequiredStage starts at 1. updateCycleScore treats a null stored required_stage as 3, which is a further hidden default.

### #39 [P1] One-click destructive deletes with no confirmation, and most deletes are not audited
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The trash icons on Parameters, Bands, Dominance, Routing and Validity submit a server action straight away, with no confirmation. When the context is live, which is the default, deleting a band or routing rule changes credit outcomes for in-flight cycles immediately. deleteScoreBand, deleteDominanceCategory, deleteRoutingRule, deleteValidityRule and deleteWeightMatrix write no audit event. upsertValidityRule is not audited either. Other audit messages carry no detail ('Weight matrix updated.', 'Routing rule saved.'). After the fact, no one can reconstruct who changed the credit policy, or what they changed.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:223-226 — immediate delete form`
- `src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:107-110 — immediate delete`
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:112-117; routing/RoutingClient.tsx:146-151; validity/ValidityClient.tsx:114-119`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:513-521, 633-641, 697-705, 758-766, 873-884 — no logAuditEvent`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:725-756 — upsertValidityRule not audited`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:693, 869 — audit text with no before/after values`

**Recommendation.** Inside drafts, put destructive actions behind ConfirmDialog with a short consequence line, or use undo-able soft deletes. Once the active version is read-only, live deletes go away entirely. Audit every mutation with version label, entity name and before → after values, for example 'v4 draft: Band "B" min 60→55'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: trash icons on Parameters, Bands, Dominance, Routing and Validity are plain form submits with no confirmation. deleteScoreBand, deleteDominanceCategory, deleteRoutingRule, deleteValidityRule and deleteWeightMatrix have no logAuditEvent, and neither does upsertValidityRule. Routing and weight-matrix audit text has no detail. deleteParameter is audited, but only with the row id. Combined with live-by-default (finding 0), a single click changes outcomes for in-flight cycles.

### #40 [P1] Irreversible admin actions (delete user, revoke role, deactivate party) fire on a single click with no confirmation or guardrails
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** One mis-click on a red trash icon hard-deletes a Supabase auth user. Clicking any role badge, whose only hint is a hover title, revokes that role immediately. That includes revoking your own founder_admin role or the last admin. Deactivating a party uses the same trash icon as delete, so an admin cannot tell soft from hard. No action previews its consequence: open cases owned by the user, committee roster membership, active cases on the party. Deleting a user who is a case RM or a roster member leaves those records orphaned; the roster already shows 'Unknown User' for them. In a credit system this can strand live cases with no owner.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:281-286 - delete-user form submits straight to adminDeleteUser, icon-only Trash2, no confirm`
- `src/app/admin/actions.ts:258 - supabaseAdmin.auth.admin.deleteUser (hard delete), no self-delete or ownership check`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:259-267 - each role Badge is a submit button for revokeRole; only affordance is title='Click to revoke'`
- `src/app/admin/actions.ts:96-111 - revokeRole has no last-founder_admin / self-revocation / final-role guard`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:186-189 - party deactivate uses Trash2 icon, no confirm`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:317 - roster renders 'Unknown User' for deleted members`

**Recommendation.** Route all four actions through one AlertDialog consequence-preview pattern (doctrine 12.13, Principle 10). Delete user: list owned open cases, roster membership and pending tasks, require reassignment or offer 'Deactivate (disable sign-in)' as the default, keep hard delete behind typing the email, and block self-delete. Revoke role: replace click-the-badge with an explicit x button that has an aria-label. Block revoking the last founder_admin on the server and warn on removing a user's final role. Deactivate party: use a Ban/Archive icon, show the count of active cases, and add a matching 'Reactivate' action.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: none of the three actions asks for confirmation. Delete user is an icon-only Trash2 that submits straight to adminDeleteUser (AdminClient.tsx:281-286). Each role Badge is a submit button for revokeRole, and its only hint is title='Click to revoke' (:259-267). revokeRole (actions.ts:96-111) has no guard against self-revocation or revoking the last founder_admin, so an admin can lock themselves out in one click. Party deactivate uses the same Trash2 icon (:186-189). The roster shows 'Unknown User' (:317) because member_ids is an array with no foreign key. REFUTED: the claim that deletion leaves cases orphaned. profiles_id_fkey cascades from auth.users, but credit_cases.rm_user_id/kam_user_id, audit_events.actor_id, stage_tasks.assigned_to and others reference profiles with no ON DELETE clause (remote_schema.sql:1259, 1334, 1339, 1559). The cascade therefore fails for any user who has worked in the system, and the admin gets a raw 'Database error deleting user' toast. Delete only succeeds for users with no references. The real problem is a cryptic failure, not stranded cases. NEW BUG the finding missed: deactivateParty returns void (actions.ts:57-66), and handleRoleAction checks res?.success, so a successful deactivation shows toast.error('Action failed'). It also throws for non-admins instead of returning an error. Downgraded to P1: the damage is recoverable (roles can be re-granted and hard delete is mostly blocked by FKs), except the last-admin lockout, which still needs the server guard. The recommendation is sound. Base 'Deactivate user' on an auth ban, not a delete, and surface FK-blocked deletes with a plain message.

### #41 [P1] Import and merge server actions are callable by any authenticated user
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm, kam

**Problem.** /admin is gated in the layout by the impersonation cookie. But processImportJob, which creates Billing Active cases and writes exposure, and handleMergeParties, which deletes parties, check only that someone is signed in. Any RM or a self-signed-up 'viewer' can call them directly. The UI is not the security boundary (doctrine 7.5), and these are the two most destructive bulk operations in the back office.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:46-48 - only `if (!user) throw`; no isAdmin`
- `src/app/admin/aliases/actions.ts:15-17 - only `if (!user) throw`; no isAdmin`
- `src/app/admin/layout.tsx:10-14 - the only admin gate is in the layout (UI layer)`
- `src/app/admin/actions.ts:127 - by contrast importPartiesCsv does check isAdmin, so the two import paths are inconsistent`

**Recommendation.** Add `if (!isAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Only Admin can …')` to processImportJob, fetchImportJobs, handleMergeParties and fetchPartiesWithAliases. Add a test that calls each one as an rm user and expects a rejection.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: processImportJob (imports/actions.ts:46-48) and handleMergeParties (aliases/actions.ts:15-17) check only authentication. The only admin gate is AdminLayout's impersonation-role check (layout.tsx:10-14). Server actions can be invoked by action ID regardless of the layout, and proxy.ts only checks that a user is signed in. importPartiesCsv does check isAdmin (actions.ts:127), so the two paths are inconsistent. Worse: the RLS policies 'auth_write' on parties, credit_cases, party_history, party_exposure, party_aliases and import_jobs allow ANY authenticated user to write (remote_schema.sql:1809-1845). The database is no backstop, and a signed-in viewer could write these tables directly through the Supabase client. The recommendation should add role-scoped RLS for these tables as well as server-side isAdmin checks. fetchImportJobs and fetchPartiesWithAliases are read-only and RLS already allows reads for any authenticated user, so gating them matters less. fetchGlobalAuditLog has no check either. Severity stays P1: this is a security gap rather than UX, and arguably P0 in security terms.

### #42 [P1] Data Imports gives no outcome receipt; skipped rows vanish and failed rows can't be inspected or exported
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, accounts

**Problem.** After 'Confirm & Import N rows' succeeds, the preview clears and nothing else happens: no toast, no summary, no scroll to the new job. The admin has to work out from the Recent Jobs table whether anything happened. With 'Ignore missing Party IDs' ticked, unresolved rows are skipped with `continue`, so they count as neither processed nor failed and 'processed / total' quietly comes up short. For failures the job row shows only the first error in 10px italic text truncated at 150px. The full error_details with row data is stored, but there is no way to view or download it. Correcting and re-uploading only the failed rows is therefore impossible.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:67-71 - success path clears state with no feedback`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:177, 197, 216, 234 - `if (ignoreMissing) continue;` with no skipped counter`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:349-355 - job stores processed/failed only; status 'completed' even when most rows were skipped`
- `src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:211-223 - only error_details[0] shown, text-[10px], truncate max-w-[150px]`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:41 - imported_by is fetched but never displayed in the jobs table`

**Recommendation.** Have processImportJob return {jobId, processed, skipped, failed}, record records_skipped, and show a receipt card: 'Imported 182 · Skipped 12 (party not found) · Failed 6'. Make each job row expandable into an error table (row #, key field, reason), with 'Download failed rows as CSV' that adds an error column so the file can be fixed and re-uploaded. Add an 'Imported by' column and use 'completed_with_errors' (warning) when failed > 0.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. The success path clears file/preview and the input with no toast or receipt (ImportsClient.tsx:67-71). `if (ignoreMissing) continue;` appears at actions.ts:177, 197, 216 and 234, with no skipped counter. The job update at :349-355 stores only processed/failed. Status is 'failed' only when failed === payload.length, so a file whose rows were all skipped ends 'completed' with 0/N. Only error_details[0].error is shown, at text-[10px] truncated to max-w-[150px] (:211-223). imported_by is joined (:41) but never rendered. Minor extra: j.import_type.replace('_',' ') replaces only the first underscore, so 'grandfathered cases' renders oddly. The recommendation is sound and uses existing semantic tokens (warning).

### #43 [P1] Opportunity: validate-before-commit dry run for imports (mapping, per-row status, duplicates)
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, accounts

**Problem.** The preview shows the first 5 raw rows with no validation at all. Every real check happens only after commit, row by row: whether party IDs resolve, numeric parsing, tranche JSON, whether the RM matches a user, and duplicate legal names. Imports are partial, not atomic, and that is never stated. So every mistake becomes a half-applied job that has to be cleaned up by hand. The templates also ask for 'UUID-HERE' even though the server accepts customer codes, which pushes admins toward the least human-friendly key.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:158-183 - preview = Object.keys/values of first 5 rows, no status column`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:79-117 - party/contractor resolution logic exists but only runs during commit`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:132-347 - per-row try/catch = partial import; UI never says 'partial'`
- `src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:81-83 - templates use party_id 'UUID-HERE'`

**Recommendation.** Split processImportJob into validateImport (read-only, reuses the same resolution and parsing code) and commitImport. After upload, show a per-row grid with a Valid / Warning / Error chip, the resolved party name next to each party_id, the RM match result, and a duplicate flag. Add filter chips (All / Errors / Warnings) and a header line such as '412 will import · 9 will be skipped · 3 errors. Import is partial: valid rows commit even if others fail.' Enable Commit only once the admin has reviewed the errors. Switch templates to customer_code.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. The preview is just Object.keys/values of the first 5 rows, with no status column (ImportsClient.tsx:158-183). Party and contractor resolution runs only inside the commit (actions.ts:79-117). Each row is wrapped in its own try/catch, so imports are partial, and the UI never says so. The templates use 'UUID-HERE' (:81-83), even though resolution accepts customer_code (:89-104). The opportunity is sound. A dry run is also the natural fix for findings 3, 6 and 7, so P1 is reasonable even for an opportunity. One caveat: contractor_id is resolved only by customer_code, not by UUID (:109-115), so the dry run should reuse the same resolver to avoid drift.

### #44 [P1] Admin-panel 'Import CSV' dialog ignores its own Import Type selector and duplicates the Imports page with different semantics
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The Party Master tab has its own import dialog with a 'Grandfathered Cases (Legacy Collections)' option and even shows the collections header list. The server action ignores import_type and always parses the file as party master. A collections CSV therefore fails with the misleading 'row missing a legal_name' error. The dialog also behaves differently from the Imports page. It is an all-or-nothing bulk insert, where Imports is a partial per-row upsert. It generates CUST-IMP-<uuid> codes for blank customer_code, where Imports inserts null. It plain-inserts instead of upserting, so one duplicate code fails the whole file. One task, two entry points, two sets of rules.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:409-413 - import_type select includes grandfathered_cases`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:417 - helper copy describes collections headers`
- `src/app/admin/actions.ts:123-144 - importPartiesCsv never reads import_type; hardcodes 'party_master' and bulk insert`
- `src/utils/csv.ts:55 - auto-generates CUST-IMP-<uuid> codes`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:164-171 - Imports page inserts customer_code null instead`

**Recommendation.** Delete the dialog and importPartiesCsv. Point the Party Master 'Import CSV' button to /admin/imports?type=party_master, which the deep link already supports. One pipeline means one set of validation and receipt rules.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. The dialog offers grandfathered_cases (AdminClient.tsx:410-413), and its copy lists the collections headers (:417). importPartiesCsv never reads import_type. It hardcodes 'party_master' and plain-inserts the whole parsed payload, all or nothing (actions.ts:123-153). parsePartiesCsv throws 'CSV contains a row missing a legal_name' (csv.ts:~52) and generates `CUST-IMP-${randomUUID()}` codes (csv.ts:55), while the Imports page inserts customer_code null (imports/actions.ts:164-171) and upserts on customer_code. A collections CSV therefore fails with a misleading error, and the two pipelines behave inconsistently. Removing the dialog and deep-linking to /admin/imports?type=party_master (ImportsClient already honours ?type) is the right fix.

### #45 [P1] Grandfathered-case import silently assigns unmatched RMs to the importing admin and reinterprets 'overdue_date' as billing date
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm, accounts

**Problem.** When rm_name doesn't exactly match a profile full_name (case-insensitive), the case's rm_user_id quietly becomes the admin running the import. Legacy collections then land in the founder's queue and out of the real RM's, and nobody is told. The rm_matched flag is stored but never shown. The template column 'overdue_date' is written to billing_date, and a bare number is read as 'days ago'. Credit-day and aging calculations in Collections therefore rest on a field whose meaning the admin never confirmed. Contractor codes that don't resolve become null, which flips case_scenario to customer_pays without warning.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:242-248 - rmId falls back to user.id (the importer)`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:316 - rm_matched computed and stored, never surfaced`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:251-260 - overdue_date to billing_date; numeric values treated as days-ago`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:238, 300 - unresolved contractor becomes null and changes case_scenario`
