NotificationDropdown: a custom div panel with no focus management and items as plain divs. cases/page.tsx:203-223 nests <Link><Button>, which is invalid interactive nesting. SearchBar has only a placeholder. Log Out is a destructive-styled outline button that is always visible. The recommendation fits the existing shadcn/Radix setup (the Button asChild pattern is already used on the home page).

### #75 [P2] The RM gets no preview of the likely outcome: no score, no terms, no due dates, and 'Stage N' is unexplained
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam

**Problem.** Before committing, the RM's only outcome signal is 'Required review depth: Stage 2 before approval'. It gives no reason (describeRoutingOutcome already exists but is unused) and uses internal stage jargon the RM persona should not need. It also does not mention that score rules cannot match at intake, so depth may grow after submit. The RM's graded intake answers produce no indicative score or band. Requested terms are never compared with what policy would recommend, and there is no tranche due-date preview. The RM cannot tell whether to trim the ask or extend days before submitting, so that learning happens later as a return or counter-offer from the KAM.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:393-396 - evaluateRequiredStage without score; reason discarded`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:860-863 - 'Stage {requiredStage} before approval' with no reason`
- `src/utils/routing.ts:24-28 - score_below rules never match at intake (depth can deepen later)`
- `src/utils/routing.ts:45-65 - describeRoutingOutcome available but not imported by the form`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:656-659 - composite days only; no per-tranche due dates`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:728-729 - 'Stage 1 Intake Questions' jargon`

**Recommendation.** Replace the row with plain language built from describeRoutingOutcome, for example 'Needs senior review because requested exposure ≥ ₹10,00,000. Could need board review if the score comes in low.' Add per-tranche 'due ~DD MMM' (assuming billing today, clearly labelled as an estimate). Retitle step 4 'Policy questions'. See the live decision rail opportunity for the step-change version.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: The facts are correct. Line 393-396 calls evaluateRequiredStage without a score. Line 862 shows 'Stage {requiredStage} before approval' with no reason. describeRoutingOutcome is not imported by the form; submitCase uses it only for the audit log. score_below rules never match at intake (routing.ts:24-28). Only composite days are shown, and line 728 reads 'Stage 1 Intake Questions'. Downgraded from P1 to P2: the routing row is accurate for what can be known at intake, and the missing score, terms and due-date preview is an enhancement rather than a defect. Adding the plain-language reason via describeRoutingOutcome is cheap and sound. describeRoutingOutcome's own copy ('Routed to Stage N: scenario is customer name...') would also need rewording for RMs.

### #76 [P2] Linear gating blocks legitimate partial work, and disabled Continue buttons never say why
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm

**Problem.** Steps are strictly sequential. A future step is unreachable (pointer-events-none, 40% opacity) until every field on earlier steps is valid. An RM who knows the site and KAM but not the final amount cannot record them. Continue buttons go disabled with no explanation of what is missing. For example, on step 2 the Continue is disabled when exposure exceeds the credit limit or when exposure is 0, and only some of these cases show a message. A credit-limit breach is a dead end: Continue is blocked and the RM is shown no path such as a limit-increase request, an exception, or who to contact. The limit is also checked against the billed party ('customer…' scenarios) even when the contractor pays, which policy should confirm.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:375-391 - canGoNext rules gate every step`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:415, 423, 427 - future steps disabled and dimmed`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:543, 670, 720 - Continue disabled without a reason`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:382-387, 598-603 - credit-limit breach blocks progress with no recovery path`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:384, 599 and src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:60 - limit evaluated on the billed party, not the payer, for customer_name_contractor_pays`

**Recommendation.** Let RMs move freely between steps and show per-step status in the stepper (done, has blockers, untouched). Keep Continue enabled. On click, if blockers exist, show a section summary that links each one and focuses the first. Keep hard validation for Submit only. For a limit breach, show headroom (limit minus outstanding) and either an explicit 'Request limit exception' path or a clear 'policy hard stop: contact X'. Confirm with policy whether the limit should apply to the paying party.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. canGoNext (375-391) gates every step, and future steps get pointer-events-none and opacity-40 (415-427). Continue buttons (543, 670, 720) carry no reason. Step 2 does show messages for a credit-limit breach (598-603) and for unreconciled tranches (662-664). There is no message when the bill or exposure is 0, and none on steps 1 or 3. 'Exposure > bill' cannot happen because the percentage is clamped to 100. A limit breach offers no recovery; the RM can only save a draft, which is itself a dead end per finding 0. For customer_name_contractor_pays the limit is evaluated on the customer, both client-side (384, 599) and on the server (actions.ts:60). Whether that is wrong is a policy question, so the 'confirm with policy' framing is right. The recommendation is sound.

### #77 [P2] Grade feedback is misleading: every mapped grade shows in green, and out-of-band values are silently ungraded
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** 'Auto-mapped to Grade N' always renders in text-success, even for the weakest grade, so a bad answer looks like a good outcome. When a numeric or date value falls outside every configured band, the grade is cleared with no message. The required check still treats the raw value as answered, and the task is then created Completed with grade null. The score silently omits that parameter and nobody is told it needs manual grading. Option labels also expose raw '(Grade N)' numbers to the RM rather than policy grade labels.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:811-815 - text-success for any mapped grade`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:268-272 - no band found means grade_value undefined, with no feedback`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:305-307 - raw value alone satisfies the required check`
- `src/utils/engine.ts:318-320 - status Completed with grade_value null`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:771 - '(Grade {m.grade})' in option text`

**Recommendation.** Show the mapped result using the policy grade_label (gradeScale already loaded) and a neutral or severity token based on its position in the scale (higher means better). When no band matches, show a warning such as 'Outside policy bands, a reviewer will grade this manually', and either keep the task Pending or flag it for review. Prefer grade labels over bare numbers in RM-facing options.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. Line 812 uses text-success for every mapped grade. When no band matches, grade_value is set to undefined with no message (268-272). The required check accepts the raw value alone (305-307). engine.ts:318-322 then creates the task as Completed with grade null. Option text shows '(Grade N)' (771). Context: the workspace completeTask applies the same band logic and also saves null when nothing matches, so silent ungrading happens app-wide, not only at intake. The recommendation to use grade_label with a position-based semantic token (higher=better) fits the conventions.

### #78 [P2] The final review is incomplete and partly wrong, and is buried under the question list
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm

**Problem.** Step 4 combines possibly dozens of question cards with the review summary, so the summary sits at the bottom of a long scroll, against the doctrine's separate 'intake questions' and 'review and submit' steps. The summary always shows a Customer row, so a contractor-only case shows a warning-toned 'Not selected'. It omits the justification, site address, per-tranche amounts and days, the count of questions answered and reused, and has no Edit links back to steps. The closing copy says 'Stage 1 tasks are created', but the engine creates tasks for all three stages, and it names neither the KAM nor an expected timeline.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:726-828 - questions and review share step 4`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:838-841 - Customer row unconditional (needsCustomer ignored)`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:833-868 - no justification, address, tranche detail, question counts or edit links`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:869-871 - 'Stage 1 tasks are created'`
- `src/utils/engine.ts:343-345 - generateAllCycleTasks creates stages 1-3`

**Recommendation.** Split into step 4 'Policy questions' (with an 'n of m answered, k reused' header) and step 5 'Review & submit'. Show the Customer row only when needsCustomer is true. Add justification, site address, a tranche table with amount, days and estimated due date, and question counts, each section with an 'Edit' link. Rewrite the consequence line as 'Goes to <KAM name> for review · first tasks due in ~N days (SLA) · you will be notified if returned'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. Questions and the review summary share step 4 (726-872). The Customer row is unconditional (838-841), so contractor-only cases show a warning-toned 'Not selected'. Justification, address, tranche detail, question counts and edit links are all absent. Line 870 says 'Stage 1 tasks are created' while generateAllCycleTasks creates stages 1-3 (engine.ts:343-345). Only Stage 1 SLAs start, so the copy is imprecise rather than outright wrong; the receipt (CaseWorkspace:139) repeats it. The recommendation is sound. The SLA/timeline line would need data the form does not currently load.

### #79 [P2] Question and party-detail loading have no loading, error or race handling, and a misleading empty state
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm

**Problem.** Intake questions reload on every scenario change with no loading indicator and no stale-response guard. If the RM switches scenario quickly, an older response can overwrite the newer one. While loading, or when the action returns [] (including when there is no active policy), step 4 shows 'No specific intake questions required for this scenario'. That is a false statement of absence, and submit will then fail with 'No active policy'. If fetchPartyDetails throws, isLoading is never reset and the party select stays disabled on 'Loading details...' with no recovery.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:180-186 - fetch on scenario change without loading, abort or ordering guard`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:731-732 - empty-state copy shown during loading or failure`
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:275-281 - no active policy returns []`
- `src/utils/engine.ts:146-148 - submit then throws 'No active policy version found'`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:104-133, 135-164 - no try/finally; setIsLoading(false) skipped on throw`

**Recommendation.** Track tasksState (loading, ready, error) and ignore stale responses (request id or AbortController). Show a skeleton while loading, and distinguish 'This scenario has no policy questions' from 'Couldn't load policy questions - Retry'. Return an explicit error when no policy is active, and show it at step 1 so the RM is not stopped at submit. Wrap party detail fetches in try/finally with an inline retry.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. The scenario effect (180-186) has no loading state or stale guard. When rmTasks is empty, step 4 shows 'No specific intake questions required…' (731-732). With no active policy, fetchRmIntakeTasks returns [] (281), and submit later throws in engine.ts:141-143. In production that error reaches the RM as the sanitized generic message. The party handlers (104-164) have no try/finally, so a throw leaves the select disabled on 'Loading details...'. The race in practice is low-likelihood, since tasks usually load before the RM reaches step 4. The no-policy false empty state is the more material issue. P2 stands.

### #80 [P2] Accessibility gaps: unlinked labels, unfocused errors, and number inputs that accept negatives
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm

**Problem.** Most labels have no htmlFor, so screen readers announce unnamed fields and clicking a label does not focus its control. This affects customer party, contractor, bill amount, city code, site ID, site address, justification and every intake question (whose inputs already have ids). Error alerts appear with role=alert, but focus stays on the button, and the tranche reconciliation error is not tied to its inputs with aria-describedby. Bill, tranche value and days fields accept negative numbers. Disabled steps use 40% opacity on muted text, which risks contrast failure. In MentionInput (the natural component for a KAM handoff note), the suggestion dropdown has no keyboard navigation, no listbox or option roles, and no pending state, so a double-click posts twice.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:465, 504, 556, 683, 690, 695, 707 - <label> without htmlFor`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:737 - task label not linked to id intake-task-${task.id} (762, 787, 801)`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:364-373, 662-664 - errors not focused or described`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:557, 621-622 - type=number without min=0`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:423 - opacity-40 disabled steps`
- `src/components/MentionInput.tsx:73-86 - mouse-only suggestion list; 50-60 no submitting state or error handling`

**Recommendation.** Add htmlFor and id pairs to every field, or switch to the shadcn Label and FormField pattern. When an error appears, move focus to the alert or the first invalid field and set aria-invalid and aria-describedby. Add min=0 (and step) to monetary and day inputs and validate on blur. Render locked steps with a visible lock icon and a sufficient-contrast token instead of opacity. Give MentionInput arrow, Enter and Esc handling, role=listbox and option roles, aria-activedescendant, and a disabled-while-posting state.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. The labels at 465, 504, 556, 683, 690, 695 and 707 lack htmlFor, and the task label at 737 is not linked to the existing intake-task ids. errorAlert has role=alert, but focus is not moved, and the tranche error at 662-664 has no aria-describedby. The number inputs at 557, 621 and 622 have no min. Locked steps use opacity-40. MentionInput (73-86) is mouse-only buttons with no listbox semantics, and handleSubmit (50-60) has no pending/disabled state or error handling. MentionInput is outside intake today, so that part belongs to comments, not intake. The recommendation is sound.

### #81 [P2] The decision brief leaves out the score drivers, so approvers have to rebuild the decision from the Work tab
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, board_member, founder_admin

**Problem.** The brief shows terms, tranches, party track record and missing inputs, but none of the parameter grades, the reviewers' reasoning, or the per-stage scores that produced the recommendation. To check why the score is 58, the approver has to switch to Work, expand past stages one at a time, and read 'Grade: 3 · reason' lines with no labels. Approve also isn't warned or blocked when the brief lists missing required inputs, and the confirmation doesn't repeat them. Comments relevant to the decision sit in a separate Conversation tab. There's no 'what changed since the last round' view for appeals or resubmissions after a return.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:120-234 — brief contents: no grades, reasons or score breakdown`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:222-231 vs 330-332 — missing inputs listed, yet Approve stays a plain primary button`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/StagesTab.tsx:252-253 — grades shown as a bare 'Grade: N' with no label or scale`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/StagesTab.tsx:82,159-172 — past stages are collapsed by default`

**Recommendation.** Add a 'Score drivers' section to the brief: the top 3 positive and top 3 negative parameters (grade label, weight, contribution, reviewer reason, grader name), stage scores, and the band thresholds near this score. If required inputs are missing, require the approver to acknowledge them before Approve is enabled. For appeal and revision rounds, show a 'Changed since round N' diff. Link each driver to its task.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: the brief (ApprovalsTab:120-234) has no grades, reasons, stage scores or drivers. Grades show as a bare 'Grade: N' (StagesTab:252). Approve is not gated on missing inputs. Overstated: (1) The missing-inputs warning almost never fires with a round open, because Submit and early approval both require the stage to be complete, and force-ready is unreachable from the UI. So 'Approve isn't blocked when inputs are missing' is mostly theoretical. (2) The final stage's tasks are not collapsed during review: active_stage is unchanged, so that stage is still isCurrent and expanded. Only earlier stages collapse. Downgraded to P2: the information exists and the gap is how much effort it takes to reach. The 'Score drivers' recommendation is sound.

### #82 [P2] Reviewers can't grade well: no grade scale meaning, no prior value, no evidence field, and misleading 'pts' copy
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* bdo, accounts, kam, founder_admin

**Problem.** Each pending task renders a full inline form, so a stage with eight tasks becomes a wall of forms. The form shows the rubric, but grade options taken from auto_band_config show only 'Value (Grade N)' without saying which direction is better. No previous-cycle answer or party-history value appears next to the input. Non-scoring tasks get only a 'Complete task' button, with no evidence link or note. The weight line says the task 'contributes up to weight×topGrade pts of this stage's score', but the case score is normalized to 0–100 against the model maximum, so these 'pts' don't match anything the reviewer sees. The task list shows task.description but not the parameter name or owning role, so a reviewer can't easily find 'my' tasks.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/StagesTab.tsx:258-260 — a TaskCompleteForm is mounted for every eligible task at once`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:73-77 — grade options lack a polarity hint (convention: higher is better)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:91-93 — 'contributes up to … pts' copy`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:7-8 — score normalized by configured_model_max_total`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:66-105 — non-scoring tasks have no note or evidence input`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:91 — owning role is fetched but StagesTab never shows it`

**Recommendation.** Collapse each task to a row (question, owner role, SLA, current answer) and open one focused panel on click, with Next / Previous task navigation. In the panel show the grade scale with 'higher = safer', the prior cycle's answer and grader, and relevant imported party data. Replace the 'pts' line with the effect on the 0–100 case score, e.g. 'Up to 12% of the case score'. Add evidence-link and note fields for non-scoring tasks. Add a 'Mine' filter that uses default_owning_role.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. A TaskCompleteForm is mounted per eligible task (StagesTab:258). Options from auto_band_config show only 'Value (Grade N)' with no polarity (TaskCompleteForm:73-74); the gradeScale path does show labels. The 'contributes up to weight×topGrade pts of this stage's score' copy doesn't match the 0–100 normalized score (scoring.ts:121). Non-scoring tasks have no note or evidence input. default_owning_role is fetched (actions.ts:91) but never displayed. There is no prior-cycle value. Downgraded to P2: this is grading ergonomics and misleading helper copy; it doesn't block anyone or mislead a decision. The recommendation is sound and consistent with higher=better.

### #83 [P2] The command bar doesn't answer 'who owns this, what's blocking, and what do I do next?'
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam, ordinary_approver, board_member, bdo, accounts

**Problem.** Owner resolves to a name only for rm and kam. For Awaiting Approval or Appealed it shows 'Unassigned · ordinary approver', which reads as if nobody has been assigned. Next action is the generic sentence for the status and is the same for every viewer, so a BDO with two overdue tasks sees 'Complete current stage tasks' rather than 'You have 2 tasks — 1 overdue'. SLA and age aren't shown even though §12.4 lists them. The header shows requested days but not requested exposure. The archive and ambiguity badges are there, but overdue-task and round-age signals are buried in the tabs.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:122-124,264-267 — owner name only for rm/kam, otherwise 'Unassigned'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:269-272 — generic meta.nextAction`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:254-261 — bill amount and requested days, no exposure or SLA`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:126-130 — railDetail is the only blocker context`

**Recommendation.** Make next action depend on the viewer: compute it from activeRole plus the tasks and rounds promises ('Your move: grade 2 tasks (1 overdue)', 'Waiting on approver — round open 3d', 'You approved; waiting on 1 more'), and put the matching CTA right there, deep-linked to the tab or task. Show the owner as a role-team label ('Credit approvers') rather than 'Unassigned', and add SLA or round age and requested exposure.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. ownerName resolves only for rm/kam (CaseWorkspace:122-124), so Awaiting Approval shows 'Unassigned' with 'ordinary approver' beneath it. Next action is the static meta.nextAction (:271), the same for every viewer. The header shows Bill amount and Requested days but no exposure, SLA or round age. P2 is right. The recommendation is sound.

### #84 [P2] The same party data shows different overdue days on different tabs, and formatting is inconsistent
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* consistency · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, kam, rm

**Problem.** Summary shows overdue days adjusted for import staleness (adjustedOverdueDays), but the Decision brief shows the raw exposure.overdue_days. An approver comparing the two sees two different numbers for the same customer. Summary also mixes raw ₹toLocaleString with an un-localized toLocaleDateString() ('Data as of' renders in browser locale, e.g. MM/DD/YYYY), while the rest of the workspace uses formatCompactINR and formatDateIST. The relaxation history uses toLocaleDateString('en-IN'). The contractor's empty state reads 'No historical exposure found', while the customer's reads 'absence of data is itself a risk signal'.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:292 — adjustedOverdueDays(…)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:199 — raw exposure.overdue_days`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:310,349 — new Date(...).toLocaleDateString() with no locale`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:174-175,285 — raw ₹ toLocaleString next to formatCompactINR in the header`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:313 vs 352 — inconsistent empty-state copy`

**Recommendation.** Extract a shared <PartyExposure party exposure history /> component used by both Summary and the Decision brief. It should apply adjustedOverdueDays everywhere, use formatINR/formatDateIST, and use the same 'absence of data is a risk signal' empty state for customer and contractor.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Facts confirmed. Summary uses adjustedOverdueDays (OverviewTab:292, 331), the brief uses raw overdue_days (ApprovalsTab:199), toLocaleDateString() has no locale (:310, 349), and raw ₹ toLocaleString and the empty-state copy are inconsistent. But the recommendation to apply adjustedOverdueDays everywhere is unsound as written. adjustedOverdueDays (dateHelpers.ts:1-7) adds the days since import even when storedOverdueDays is 0, so Summary can show '₹0 (40 days overdue)' for a party with no overdue at all. Arguably the brief's raw value is the more correct one. The shared component should adjust only when overdue_amount > 0 and overdue_days > 0, and label the result as an estimate ('≥N days, est. from import date').

### #85 [P2] Summary layout breaks on mobile: a col-span-2 card inside a one-column grid, and fixed-width controls
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* responsive · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, board_member

**Problem.** The 'Review Cycle' card uses col-span-2 (not lg:col-span-2) inside grid-cols-1, which on phones forces an implicit second column and squeezes or misaligns the Commercial and People cards. The persona selects (200, 200 and 220px) and the counter-offer input (200px) are fixed widths. The command bar's five or six outline buttons wrap into several rows above the fold on a 375px screen, pushing status and terms below the first screen. Doctrine wants mobile to keep the work (decide, vote, record acceptance), not the desktop layout.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:169,220 — grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 with child 'col-span-2'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:77,86,95,153 — fixed w-[200px]/w-[220px] controls`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:194-225 — up to six header buttons, no overflow menu`

**Recommendation.** Change it to lg:col-span-2. Use w-full sm:w-[200px] for the selects and inputs. On small screens, collapse secondary header actions (Unlock, Personas, Withdraw, Print) into a '…' menu so only the role's primary CTA stays visible, and make it sticky at the bottom on mobile for decide, vote and record acceptance.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. The Review Cycle card is `col-span-2` inside `grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2` (OverviewTab:169, 220). Spanning 2 in a one-track grid creates an implicit auto column on mobile, unlike the sibling cards, which use `col-span-1 lg:col-span-2`. The fixed w-[200px]/w-[220px] selects and the counter-offer input are there too (77, 86, 95, 127, 153). The header can render up to six buttons with flex-wrap and no overflow menu. The lg:col-span-2 fix is correct.

### #86 [P2] Opportunity: a persistent decision rail that replaces three tabs with one view
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, board_member, founder_admin, kam, rm

**Problem.** Today the decision story is spread across the header score card, Summary (ladder, commercial, people, exposure), Work (grades, stage scores), Decision (brief, rounds) and Conversation (discussion). Tabs mount lazily, so approvers switch tabs repeatedly to cross-check, and the terms are rendered in four places with different logic. Doctrine §12.4 warns against 'six equal database-oriented tabs'.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:321-382 — six peer tabs`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:278-318, OverviewTab.tsx:167,219-239, ApprovalsTab.tsx:134-162 — terms and score repeated in four places`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CommentsTab.tsx:7-51 — comments aren't tied to a task or decision`

**Recommendation.** On desktop (≥lg), add a sticky right-hand rail with the single TermsLadder, score with band, the top drivers, the open round's status (n of m decided, age, your status), and the viewer's primary CTA. On mobile, show it as a collapsible sheet. Remove the duplicate score card and Review Cycle card. Let comments be anchored to a task or round, and show the anchored thread inline in the task panel and the brief.

*Verification:* confirmed: Valid as an opportunity. Six peer tabs (CaseWorkspace:321-382). Radix unmounts inactive TabsContent, so cross-checking means switching tabs. Terms and score repeat across the score card, Summary ladder, Review Cycle card and Decision ladder. CommentsTab is case-level only. The recommendation is consistent with finding 3. Removing the duplicate cards should follow fixing the data model, the separate policy and approved columns, so the rail's single ladder is correct.

### #87 [P2] Opportunity: show live score impact while grading, and let approvers approve at adjusted terms or with conditions
*Area:* workspace-review · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* bdo, accounts, kam, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** A grader can't see how their grade moves the case score, band or recommended days until after saving and revalidating, and the current stage's score is never computed for display (stageSummaries skips the current stage). Approvers have only three choices: approve the policy terms as they are, reject, or return. Approving at fewer days or adding a condition (a PDC, a shorter first tranche) isn't possible, which forces a reject-and-resubmit cycle for common credit-ops decisions. Only the founder can go higher than the recommendation.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:115-117 — 'current calculation deferred to UI', but the UI never does it`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/StagesTab.tsx:76,119 — current stage shows no score badge`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:328-339 — only Approve, Reject or Return`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:540-543 — only an upward override, founder only`

**Recommendation.** (1) Add a client-side 'what-if' preview in the grading panel using the snapshot weights and bands: 'This grade: case 64 → 69, band B → A, recommended 30 → 45 days'. (2) Add 'Approve with changes' for ordinary approvers: lower days or a stricter tranche structure plus free-text conditions. Store it as the approved layer with provenance, and show the conditions in the negotiation dialog and the ledger. Keep the founder-only upward override as it is.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. actions.ts:115-117 defers current-stage scoring to the UI, and the UI never computes it. stageSummaries returns score=null for the current stage, so StagesTab shows no badge. Approvers get only Approve, Reject or Return (ApprovalsTab:328-339). The only change to terms is a founder-only upward override (actions.ts:540-543). It's a valid P2 opportunity. Caveat for the what-if preview: the client must mirror scoring.ts exactly, including the dominance and persona combination and checkAmbiguity, or it will show a different score from the server. It's safer to call a server-side dry-run of calculateFinalCaseScore.

### #88 [P2] The 'Apply payment to tranche' picker does nothing because the server ignores trancheIndex
*Area:* money · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* accounts, kam

**Problem.** In Collections the user can choose which overdue tranche a payment applies to ('T2 · due 12 Mar · ₹1.2L'), and the form posts trancheIndex. handleLogPayment never reads it. Allocation is always oldest-first by summing all repayments. An Accounts user who deliberately books against T2 (for example, because the customer's remittance advice says so) gets a different result from what they chose and is never told. This is the reverse of doctrine §12.9, which says to show or confirm how the waterfall will allocate.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1332-1349 — tranche selector UI`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1118 — trancheIndex posted`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:258-262 — trancheIndex never read`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:79-80 — waterfall allocates the sum of repayments sequentially`

**Recommendation.** Remove the picker and replace it with a live allocation preview under the amount field, e.g. 'This ₹2,00,000 clears T1 (₹1,50,000) and pays ₹50,000 of T2; ₹1,00,000 still overdue.' If directed allocation is a real business need, model it explicitly (repayments.tranche_index honoured by the waterfall) rather than pretending.

*Verification:* confirmed: Behavior confirmed. The picker (1332-1349) and the 'applies to T{n}' label suggest directed allocation, and trancheIndex is posted at 1118. handleLogPayment (258-262) never reads it. The waterfall (fetchLedgerData 80, page.tsx 63, client 111) allocates actual_bill_amount oldest-first. Downgraded to P2: the money and total outstanding are recorded correctly, and only the per-tranche attribution differs from what the user picked. Oldest-first is very likely the intended business rule, so the UI is misleading rather than the data being lost. The recommended fix (remove the picker, add an allocation preview) is right.

### #89 [P2] After recording a payment in Collections the queue stays stale and the receipt hides the business outcome
*Area:* money · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* accounts, kam

**Problem.** handleLogPayment only revalidates /cases/{id}, so Collections keeps showing the old outstanding, DPD, aging band, stat tiles and '% collected' until a manual refresh. The success line 'Payment of ₹X recorded.' never clears and says nothing about the outcome: which tranches cleared, the new outstanding, or that the case auto-closed and will drop off the queue. On the Ledger, auto-close also happens silently. The Billing frame form hides itself immediately on submit, before the save resolves, so a validation error appears with the form already gone.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:310 — revalidatePath only for the case`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1119-1123 — static success message, never cleared`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:325-334 — auto-close with no return value to the UI`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:280-284 — setShowBillingEdit(false) runs before wrap() resolves`

**Recommendation.** Have the payment action return {newOutstanding, clearedTranches, closed} and revalidate '/collections'. Render a receipt per §14.5: '₹2,00,000 recorded by Priya 14:32 · T1 cleared · ₹1.0L outstanding · next due 12 Dec'. If closed: 'Case settled and removed from queue', with an undo window or a link to edit. Keep the billing form open until success.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: The staleness claim is refuted. In Next 16.2.1, revalidatePath sets workStore.pathWasRevalidated whatever the path (revalidate.js:210 has a 'TODO: only revalidate if the path matches'). The action response therefore re-renders the current route, so /collections does refresh its outstanding, DPD, bands and tiles after handleLogPayment. The page is also dynamic (cookies/auth). The rest is confirmed. The success text at 1121 is static and never cleared, and the action returns nothing about cleared tranches, new outstanding or closure. If the case auto-closes, the row just unmounts and disappears with no receipt, which makes the 'silent drop-off' worse. LedgerTab 280-284 calls setShowBillingEdit(false) right after a non-awaited wrap(), so a validation error appears after the form has already gone. Downgraded to P2. Revalidating '/collections' explicitly is harmless but not needed for correctness. The receipt recommendation stands.

### #90 [P2] Opportunity: structured non-payment reasons (RCA) on contact logs and broken PTPs
*Area:* money · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, rm, founder_admin, accounts

**Problem.** Why a customer is late is the most important collections signal, and today it is buried in free text. Logs are untyped strings with regex-parsed '[call]' and '[PTP …]' markers. The admin-managed delay_reason enumeration exists but is only used when completing review tasks. Leadership cannot answer 'how much of our 90+ book is disputes vs cash-flow vs stalled sites', broken promises carry no reason, and the next credit decision for the same party cannot learn from it.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:151-167 — log type/PTP parsed out of free text with regex`
- `src/app/collections/actions.ts:149-151 — markers concatenated into the message string`
- `src/app/settings/actions.ts:7,77 — 'delay_reason' enumeration managed in settings`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:110 — delay reasons used only for task completion`

**Recommendation.** Add structured columns to hq_collection_logs (contact_type, outcome, reason_code, ptp_date, ptp_amount) instead of string markers. Show reason chips in the LogDrawer (Dispute/quality, Customer cash-flow, Awaiting end-client payment, Site stalled, Unreachable, Billing error), driven by a 'collection_delay_reason' enumeration. Require a reason when a PTP breaks. Add a 'Reason' filter and a stacked aging-by-reason bar, and feed the latest reasons into party history for the next credit review.

*Verification:* confirmed: The facts are accurate. Logs are free text with [call]/[PTP] markers parsed by regex (151-167) and built by string concatenation (actions.ts 149-151). The delay_reason enumeration (settings/actions.ts 7, 77) is used only for the SLA-breach reason in TaskCompleteForm (110) via cases/[id]/actions.ts:93. This is a product opportunity, not a defect, so P2 rather than P1. The recommendation is sound but needs a schema migration for hq_collection_logs and a new enumeration category. Use a separate category, as proposed, rather than reusing the SLA delay_reason.

### #91 [P2] Opportunity: risk-weighted 'Work next' priority instead of a pure DPD sort
*Area:* money · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, rm, accounts

**Problem.** The default ordering is worst DPD only, inside fixed bands, so a ₹5k case at 95 days sits above a ₹40L case at 85 days. Broken promises, untouched cases, PTPs due today and large party-level exposure are all available as derived data, but the user has to combine them mentally across six tiles. The board's 'Today's queue' starts empty every morning and must be filled by hand-dragging.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:370-377 — sort by worstDpd / outstanding / name only`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:802-821 — grouping strictly by DPD band`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:432-438 — board resets to backlog daily`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:172-188 — Derived already has outstanding, brokenPtp, activePtp, lastContactDays`

**Recommendation.** Add a 'Priority' sort as the default. Score = outstanding × DPD-band weight, plus boosts for broken PTP, PTP due today, untouched ≥14d and party total outstanding. Show a one-line 'why' chip per row ('Broke ₹2L PTP · 9d no contact'). Auto-seed 'Today's queue' each morning with the top N plus all PTPs due today, and let users dismiss. Keep the DPD sort available.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. The sort keys are only worstDpd, outstanding and name (370-377). The default view groups strictly by DPD band (802-821). boardColOf (432-438) honours board status only for today, so the board resets to backlog every day. Derived already has outstanding, brokenPtp, activePtp and lastContactDays. Party-total outstanding is not in Derived but can be computed from relatedCases. As an opportunity, P2 is fair. If a score is added, keep its formula visible (the 'why' chip) so the ordering stays explainable.

### #92 [P2] Ledger 'Margin' badge shows a red negative percentage while collection is still in progress
*Area:* money · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* kam, founder_admin, accounts

**Problem.** Margin is computed as collected / decided − 1 and rendered with the destructive badge whenever it is below zero. A case 30% collected shows 'Margin: −70.00%' in red, which reads as a 70% loss rather than collection still in flight. It also mixes bases: margin uses decided while progress uses promised. 'Margin' is never defined on screen, and two-decimal precision adds false accuracy.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:175-177 — margin = actual/decided − 1`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:266-273 — destructive badge when negative, toFixed(2)`
- `src/lib/vocabulary.ts:210 — decided is 'margin baseline'`

**Recommendation.** Hide realized margin until the case is closed. While Billing Active, show 'Projected margin (promised vs decided): +4.2%' in neutral, and after closure show 'Realized margin', with a tooltip giving the formula. Never use colour alone (§13.2).

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. LedgerTab 175-177 computes margin as actual/decided − 1, and 266-273 shows a destructive badge when it is below 0, with toFixed(2) and no definition. A case 30% collected shows '-70.00%' in red. The bases differ: margin uses decided, progress uses promised. GLOSSARY calls decided the 'margin baseline'. Correction: the badge does show a sign and a number, so colour is not used alone and the §13.2 citation is weak. Downgraded to P2: this is misleading labelling on a secondary metric and does not drive any action. The recommendation (hide realized margin until closed, show projected margin in neutral) is sound.

### #93 [P2] Accessibility gaps: nested interactive controls, unlabeled payment fields, dialog without focus management, native confirm/alert
*Area:* money · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* accounts, kam, rm

**Problem.** Each Collections row is a role=button div that contains a checkbox and a 'Record payment' button. Nested interactive controls confuse screen readers and make Space/Enter ambiguous. Payment amount, date and note inputs have placeholders but no labels. The LogDrawer declares role=dialog but has no aria-modal, focus trap or focus return. Ledger edit/delete payment buttons are icon-only with no aria-label. Destructive and important flows rely on window.confirm and alert, which are unstyled and easy to dismiss by reflex.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1140-1159,1211-1220 — interactive children inside role=button`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1352-1373 — unlabeled inputs`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1564-1568 — dialog without aria-modal/focus trap`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:573-588 — icon-only Pencil/Trash2 buttons`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:579,603,382 and src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:451 — confirm()/alert()`

**Recommendation.** Make the expand toggle a dedicated chevron <button> and keep the row as a non-interactive container. Add <Label htmlFor> to all payment fields. Rebuild LogDrawer on the shadcn Sheet (Radix Dialog) for focus trapping. Add aria-labels such as 'Edit payment of ₹50,000 on 12 Nov'. Replace confirm/alert with AlertDialog and inline receipts.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: the row is a role=button div (1140-1148) containing a checkbox (1150-1158) and a Record payment Button (1211-1220). The Collections payment inputs are unlabeled (1352-1373). The LogDrawer has role=dialog with no aria-modal (1564-1568). The Ledger Pencil/Trash buttons are icon-only with no aria-label (573-588). The native confirm/alert calls are at 579, 603 and 382, and CollectionsClient:451. Correction: the LogDrawer does move focus to the message field on open (1523) and closes on Escape (1525). Focus trap and focus return are still missing. Recommendation is sound.

### #94 [P2] Bulk work is limited to RM reassignment, which can look like it failed on legacy cases
*Area:* money · *Kind:* efficiency · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, accounts, founder_admin

**Problem.** Only KAM/Admin get selection, and the only bulk action is 'Assign to RM'. There is no bulk 'add to Today's queue', bulk log ('sent statement to all 12'), bulk escalate or CSV export for Accounts reconciliation. Selection is hidden in Board mode. bulkAssignRMs has no error handling or confirmation receipt. getRmName prefers case_attributes.original_rm_name, so after reassigning imported legacy cases the rows still show the old RM and the RM filter doesn't move them. The action appears to have done nothing.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:217 — canBulkAssign kam|founder_admin only`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:826-860 — single bulk action, list mode only, no error path`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:91-97 — original_rm_name overrides the assigned RM`
- `src/app/collections/actions.ts:112-126 — bulk update with no result returned`

**Recommendation.** Show the assigned RM, with 'originally: X' as secondary text for legacy cases. Return a receipt ('12 cases reassigned to Ravi'). Add bulk 'Move to Today', 'Log contact', 'Escalate' and 'Export CSV (exact amounts, due dates, DPD, last contact, PTP)', and make Accounts eligible for export and selection.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. canBulkAssign is kam|founder_admin (217). The only bulk action is Assign to RM, and only in list mode (827). The form action awaits bulkAssignRMs with no try/catch or receipt (835-839), and the server returns nothing (112-126). getRmName (91-97) prefers case_attributes.original_rm_name, and both the RM filter (364) and search use rmName. After reassigning legacy cases the rows still show the old RM and don't move under the filter, so the action looks like a no-op. There is no CSV export (the only CSV mention is the import link at 607). Recommendation is sound.

### #95 [P2] The Ledger has no single next action and mixes in pre-approval content, while some exception tools have no UI
*Area:* money · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam, accounts

**Problem.** Ownership of the billing handoff is described in a prose paragraph rather than a stepper showing who is pending (RM handover → KAM decided amount → Accounts invoice → payments). Nobody sees 'You are up: attach invoice'. A large branded 'Contractor Reputation Checks' card with field-diligence instructions (a pre-approval activity) appears on every post-approval Ledger for every role, competing with money data. handleRestructureTranches is imported but has no UI, so KAMs cannot reschedule tranches within MAX_TRANCHE_EXTENSION_DAYS and fall back to PTPs or escalation. Labels drift as well: 'RM Handover Date' in the form vs 'Billing Date' in the summary.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:202-204 — handoff sequence as static text`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:723-765 — reputation checklist card on the Ledger`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:24 — handleRestructureTranches imported, never rendered`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/billing-actions.ts:675-724 — restructure action with extension guard`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:234 vs 289 — 'Billing Date' vs 'RM Handover Date'`

**Recommendation.** Replace the paragraph with a 4-step handoff stepper showing owner, status and a primary CTA for the active role. Move the reputation checklist to the Stages/review context, or collapse it behind a KAM-only disclosure. Add a 'Reschedule tranche' dialog that shows original vs new due date and the remaining extension allowance, with a required reason. Unify the date label as 'Billing (handover) date'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. The handoff is a static paragraph (202-204). The Contractor Reputation Checks card (723-765) renders unconditionally for every role and status on the Ledger. handleRestructureTranches is imported (24) but never rendered, while the action at 675-724 enforces MAX_TRANCHE_EXTENSION_DAYS. The labels disagree: 'Billing Date' (234) vs 'RM Handover Date' (289). Also, the KAM decided-amount form (310-324) stays visible even after the amount is set, which adds to the lack of a clear next action. Recommendation is sound.

### #96 [P2] The upcoming-due horizon is a read-only dead end
*Area:* money · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, rm, accounts

**Problem.** The 7/14/30-day views exist to prevent overdues, but each item is just a link to the case. There is no 'log pre-due call', no PTP, no payment entry, no reminder and no board. Search, RM filter and stats disappear in this mode, and the header loses the List/Board toggle, so a KAM with 60 cases due this fortnight cannot filter to their RM or record outcomes inline. The proactive workflow §12.10 asks for is shown but not supported.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:495-545 — separate early return, rows are plain Links`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:478 — counts ignore filters`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:516-538 — no actions per row`

**Recommendation.** Render the upcoming horizon through the same list pipeline: filters, search, and CaseRow with the contact log, record payment and a 'Confirmed with customer' quick log. Add a 'Due this week' total tile. Consider a single timeline sorted from −90d to +30d with a 'Today' divider instead of a mode switch.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed. The upcoming horizon is a separate early return (495-545) whose rows are plain Links with no actions, search, RM filter, stats or List/Board toggle. The counts (478) come from the unfiltered upcomingCases. upcomingCases also excludes cases without decided_bill_amount (page.tsx:94), and hqLogs and relatedCases are fetched only for overdue IDs (page.tsx 121, 156). Reusing CaseRow would need those queries widened. Recommendation is sound, and it overlaps with finding 0's early-payment gap.

### #97 [P2] Validity rules point at bands by name, so renaming a band silently orphans them; rule precedence is invisible
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, kam

**Problem.** The validity rule's score_band stores the band's display name. Renaming 'Band A' to 'Band A — Prime' leaves the rule pointing at a band that no longer exists. Matching then falls through to a less specific rule or to none, and the approval-expiry window changes without anyone noticing. When several rules match, the engine picks the most specific rule first and then the shortest window. The page lists rules by created_at and never explains that order, so the admin cannot predict which window a case will get.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/validity/ValidityClient.tsx:63 — option value={band.band_name}`
- `src/utils/validity.ts:14 — string equality on score_band`
- `src/utils/validity.ts:19-23 — precedence: specificity, then shortest days`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:496 — band update does not cascade to validity_rules`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:717-721 — rules ordered by created_at`

**Recommendation.** Reference bands by stable id, or cascade renames. Flag orphaned rules in red and count them in the publish checklist. Sort rules in evaluation order and add a line such as 'Most specific rule wins; ties take the shorter window'. Add an inline tester (band × scenario → 'Rule #2 applies: 60 days'), or better, a band × scenario grid that shows the effective window in every cell.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: the option value is band.band_name (ValidityClient.tsx:63). validity.ts:14 uses string equality. Precedence is specificity first, then the shortest window (19-23). upsertScoreBand does not cascade to validity_rules. fetchValidityRules orders by created_at desc. Severity lowered: the page states validity runs in 'Warn mode', where expiry never blocks negotiation or acceptance, and band renames are infrequent. The impact is a wrong non-blocking expiry stamp, so P2.

### #98 [P2] Auto-mapping grades and stages are hard-coded (1–5, Stage 1–3) and ignore the configured Grade Scale
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* consistency · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm, bdo

**Problem.** Grade Scales lets the admin define any labels and values, and the task form and stage capacity use them. The parameter editor still hard-codes 'Manual Grade (1-5)', 'Map raw values to a 1-5 grade', a 1–5 select for mappings, and Yes=5/No=1 defaults. On a 1–10 scale, 'Yes → 5' is a middling grade. Mappings also show bare numbers instead of grade labels, so admins have to remember what '3' means. Stage filters and pickers are fixed at 1–3 on every page. The table column titled 'Category' shows Stage. The version badge on Parameters is always green (success), even for a draft or archived version.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:283, 352, 409 — hard-coded 1–5`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:80, 278 — Yes=5/No=1 defaults`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:18-22, 76 — task form uses grade_scale`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:180 — 'Category' header for stage`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:142 — Badge variant='success' for any version`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:164; routing/RoutingClient.tsx:100-102; stages/StagesClient.tsx:45-47`

**Recommendation.** Pass the version's grade_scale into ParametersClient. Render mapping selects as 'value — label', and default Yes and No to the top and bottom configured grades. Rename the column to 'Stage'. Drive the version badge from the same status logic as PolicyContextBar. Grade Scales should also show a uniqueness check on values and warn when values change in a version that is already in use.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: ParametersClient has 'Manual Grade (1-5)' (283), 'Map raw values to a 1-5 grade' (352), a [1..5] mapping select (409), and Yes=5/No=1 defaults (80, 278). The stage column header reads 'Category' (180) and the version Badge is always variant='success' (142). TaskCompleteForm derives topGrade from data.gradeScale. Stage pickers are hard-coded 1-3 everywhere, but the DB CHECK constrains stage to 1-3, so that part is consistent with the schema and not a defect.

### #99 [P2] The IA is 10 CRUD islands with two different page patterns and no persistent navigation; layouts break on narrow screens
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** Policy is one model, but it is split across ten pages. Each needs a round trip through the hub, and the pages use two unrelated patterns. Parameters, Grades, Bands and Personas have a Back link, a header Add button and Dialog editing. Weights, Stages, Dominance, Routing, Validity and Simulation have no Back link and use a left form with a right table. That second group hard-codes grid-cols-3 or grid-cols-2 with no breakpoint, so on a tablet or phone the form column becomes about 100px wide. To answer something like 'why does a 70-score contractor case get 30 days?', an admin has to visit Parameters, Weights, Stages, Dominance and Bands in turn, keeping numbers in their head.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/page.tsx:19-40 — 10 destinations, all reached only through the hub`
- `src/app/policy/weights/WeightsClient.tsx:16-19 — no back link`
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:39; routing/RoutingClient.tsx:56; stages/StagesClient.tsx:33; validity/ValidityClient.tsx:50 — grid-cols-3 with no responsive prefix`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:58 — grid-cols-2 with no responsive prefix`
- `src/app/policy/layout.tsx:16 — layout adds no shared navigation`

**Recommendation.** Give policy a shared layout: a left rail or tabs following the pipeline order, with a version pill and the Live/Draft bar always visible. Then use one editing grammar everywhere: table plus side sheet. Use `grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3` throughout. Merge closely coupled pages: Grades into Parameters (both are evidence definitions), Stage Totals into Weights (derived), and Routing plus Validity into one 'Lifecycle rules' page.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: only Parameters, Grades, Bands and Personas have Back links. Weights, Stages, Dominance, Routing, Validity and Simulation have none. Dominance, Routing, Stages and Validity use `grid grid-cols-3` with no breakpoint, and Simulation uses `grid-cols-2`. policy/layout.tsx only does a role gate and returns children, with no shared navigation. The merge suggestions are opinion, but reasonable.

### #100 [P2] Opportunity: a model-centric 'Score anatomy' view with live health checks on the hub
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* founder_admin, board_member

**Problem.** The hub's cards show only static descriptions, and its version rows only carry counts of four tables. No single screen shows the scoring model as a pipeline: evidence → grade → each parameter's effective share of the stage score per subject and persona → normalization → dominance → band → recommended days → required stage and validity. Admins cannot see weight concentration, for example one parameter carrying 40% of the Stage 1 customer score. They also cannot see broken links between steps (band gaps, stage drift, orphaned validity rules, unused personas) without visiting every page.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/page.tsx:19-40 — static card copy, no status`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:28-40 — fetchPolicyVersionCounts is the only aggregate`
- `src/app/policy/weights/WeightsClient.tsx:43,62-65 — raw weights only, no % share and no subject split`

**Recommendation.** Make /policy show one horizontal pipeline diagram for the selected version. Each step is a node with a status chip, for example 'Bands: gap 59–60', 'Stage 2 totals drift 12%', 'All good'. Clicking a node opens that editor. Add a 'Weight share' panel: stacked bars per stage × subject × persona showing each parameter's percentage contribution at top grade. Add a 'Policy explainer' export, a one-page plain-language summary of the model for board members.

*Verification:* confirmed: Opportunity. The evidence holds: hub cards are static copy, fetchPolicyVersionCounts is the only aggregate, and WeightsClient shows raw weights with no share and no subject split. The recommendation depends on the validators from findings 3, 4 and 8.

### #101 [P2] Opportunity: back-test the draft against real cycles before publishing
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* founder_admin, board_member

**Problem.** The doctrine's design test asks: 'Can an admin predict what cases will be affected… before publishing?' Today the answer is no. The data to answer it already exists: stage_tasks hold grade_value for every completed cycle, and routing.ts and validity.ts are already pure functions. What is missing is a pure scoring core and a screen to show the results.

**Evidence.**
- `src/utils/routing.ts:33-43 — pure evaluateRequiredStage, reusable`
- `src/utils/validity.ts:11-26 — pure selectValidityRule, reusable`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:12-75 — scoring tied to Supabase queries per call (needs extracting into pure functions)`
- `src/app/policy/PolicyVersionActions.tsx:29-32 — the publish dialog has nowhere to show impact`

**Recommendation.** Add 'Impact preview (last 90 days / open cycles)' to the draft hub and the Publish Review page. Re-score each cycle's captured grades under live and under the draft, then show three things. First, a band migration matrix. Second, the Δ in average recommended credit days, plus exposure-weighted days at risk. Third, cases whose required stage or ambiguity flag changes. Each cell should drill down to the list of cases. Store the summary with the publish audit event.

*Verification:* confirmed: Opportunity. The doctrine quote is real (CREDITFLOW_PRODUCT_UX_DESIGN_DOCTRINE.md:1043). evaluateRequiredStage and selectValidityRule are pure. Scoring is coupled to Supabase on every call. The publish dialog has nowhere to show impact. The recommendation is feasible because stage_tasks store grade_value.

### #102 [P2] Archived versions still show every edit control, which fails with raw errors; Routing and Validity use alert()
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The context bar says an archived version is 'read-only history', but every page still shows Add, Edit, Delete and the weight cells. Clicking them calls an action that throws ''v2' is archived…'. In Dialog pages that throw falls through to the error boundary after the dialog has already closed. Routing and Validity catch errors and show a generic browser alert ('Failed to save routing rule') that drops the actual reason. The weight matrix's Save and Clear have no pending state, so double-clicks create duplicate override rows.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:75-77 — the server throws for archived versions`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:149,222-226 — controls always rendered`
- `src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:53,106-110 — same`
- `src/app/policy/routing/RoutingClient.tsx:38-40; validity/ValidityClient.tsx:30-32 — alert()`
- `src/app/policy/weights/WeightsClient.tsx:51-58 — no pending or disabled state; insert path has no uniqueness check (actions.ts:866-867)`

**Recommendation.** Pass `readOnly = !is_draft` (or `!is_draft && !is_active` until live becomes read-only) into every client. Hide or disable mutation controls, and replace the empty-state CTA with 'Create a draft from this version'. Show errors inline with the server's message, using a shared form-error component. Add pending states, and a unique constraint on (persona_id, parameter_id).

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: no client receives a readOnly or is_draft prop, so archived versions render every mutation control. The server throws the 'archived' error, which reaches the error boundary from form actions. Routing and Validity catch it and alert() a generic message. The WeightsClient Save and Clear buttons have no pending or disabled state. weight_matrices has no unique constraint on (persona_id, parameter_id) in the schema, so a double submit on the insert path can create duplicate rows.

### #103 [P2] Opportunity: one-click 'Revert job' and idempotent re-import
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** Every imported history, exposure and grandfathered-case row is already tagged with import_job_id, but nothing in the UI uses it to roll a bad job back. Cleanup means editing the database by hand. Re-uploading the same historical file inserts duplicate party_history rows, because it uses insert and not an upsert keyed on party and data_as_of. If processImportJob throws between creating the job and the final update, the job stays 'processing' (amber) for good, because there is no try/finally.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:185, 205, 312 - import_job_id persisted on every row`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:183 - party_history.insert (not upsert) means duplicates on re-run`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:69-75 vs 349-355 - job created 'processing'; final update not in finally`
- `src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:207 - non-completed/failed status renders as warning forever`

**Recommendation.** Add a 'Revert' action on each job row, allowed within N days and only while no repayment is logged on grandfathered cases. It should preview the counts ('Will remove 182 history rows, 40 cases') and then delete by import_job_id in one RPC, with an audit entry. Upsert history and exposure on (party_id, data_as_of). Wrap the job loop in try/finally so it always ends 'failed' or 'completed'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. import_job_id is written at :185, :205 and :312, and nothing in the UI uses it to revert. party_history and party_exposure use plain insert (:183, :203), so re-runs create duplicates. The job is created as 'processing' (:69-75) and the final update (:349-355) is not in a finally block. ImportsClient renders anything that is neither completed nor failed as warning forever (:207). Per-row errors are caught, but there is a concrete path to a stuck job: profileNameMap is built with p.full_name.toLowerCase() (:123-125), which throws a TypeError for any profile with a null full_name (AdminClient shows 'No name', so these exist). That leaves grandfathered jobs stuck in 'processing'. Caveat on the recommendation: the existing unique key for party_history upserts is (party_id, import_job_id) (actions.ts:382), so an upsert on (party_id, data_as_of) needs a new constraint and a check against recompute semantics.

### #104 [P2] Vocabulary and ID-prefix managers: inactive items look active, delete sits next to deactivate, prefixes change with no preview or validation
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** In both reason lists a deactivated reason looks identical to an active one, with no badge or dimming. The only clue is that the button says 'Activate' in green instead of a red 'Deactivate'. Red 'Deactivate' text sits right beside a trash icon that hard-deletes, possibly a reason that historical cases already reference, and the two are hard to tell apart. Prefix Manager saves any string, including an empty one, with no example of the resulting ID ('CF-RPR-0001'), no success feedback, and a stale-closure bug in the loading-id set. City-code delete uses a native confirm() and doesn't check whether site IDs already use the code.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/settings/RcaReasonManager.tsx:62-77 - identical row styling regardless of is_active; Deactivate (text-destructive) beside Delete`
- `src/app/settings/DelayReasonManager.tsx:62-77 - same pattern`
- `src/app/settings/actions.ts:100, 113 - hard delete of enumeration rows`
- `src/app/settings/PrefixManager.tsx:19-29 - no validation, no success toast, `new Set(loadingIds)` uses stale state`
- `src/app/settings/actions.ts:150-158 - updateIdPrefix accepts any string`
- `src/app/settings/CityCodeManager.tsx:35-36 - native confirm(), no in-use check`

**Recommendation.** Show an 'Inactive' badge and muted text on deactivated reasons, sorted below active ones. Turn Deactivate into a neutral Switch and keep Delete only for reasons never referenced, otherwise disabled with a tooltip explaining why. In Prefix Manager, validate with a 2-6 uppercase-letter regex, show a live 'Next ID will look like …' preview, use a functional setState, toast on success, and confirm with 'Existing IDs will not change; new IDs will use X'. Use AlertDialog with usage counts for city-code delete.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: rows are styled the same whatever is_active is, and red 'Deactivate' sits beside a Trash2 delete (RcaReasonManager.tsx:62-77; DelayReasonManager same). Delete is a hard delete (actions.ts:100, 113), though it IS guarded by window.confirm, which the finding omits, and the button has an aria-label. PrefixManager has no validation and no success feedback, and has the stale closure: `new Set(loadingIds)` at :26 reads render-time state and can clear another row's loading flag during concurrent saves. City-code delete uses native confirm() (CityCodeManager.tsx:36) with no in-use check, and its icon button lacks an aria-label. Refuted part of the recommendation: an empty prefix is a supported state. idEngine.getPrefix returns '' and generateContractorId only appends '/' when prefix is truthy (idEngine.ts:69-80). The separator is '/', not '-', so the 'CF-RPR-0001' example and the mandatory 2-6 uppercase regex would contradict existing behaviour. Allow empty, and base the preview on idEngine's actual format.

### #105 [P2] Committee roster: unsaved edits silently lost, no quorum guidance, stale members shown as 'Unknown User'
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin, board_member

**Problem.** Add and remove only change local state, yet nothing shows unsaved changes. Switching tabs or leaving the page drops them, and an admin can believe the roster was updated when it wasn't. Save accepts any size, including zero, with no guidance about the board size that board review needs (a 7-person board per product notes), so ambiguity reviews can be left without quorum. Deleted users appear as 'Unknown User' with a raw UUID. If no one is eligible, the 'Eligible Board Members' list renders as an empty bordered box with no explanation that the board_member role is needed first.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:34, 324, 360 - local-only state mutations`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:332-341 - Save/Reset with no dirty indicator or leave guard`
- `src/app/admin/actions.ts:280-315 - updateCommitteeRoster accepts any memberIds length`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:313-318 - 'Unknown User' fallback`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:346-365 - no empty state for eligible list`

**Recommendation.** Show a 'N unsaved changes' bar with the Save button and a beforeunload/tab-switch guard. Show 'Members: 5 of 7 required' with a warning token below quorum, and enforce the minimum on the server from system_settings. Flag deleted or role-less members with a destructive badge and a Remove action. Add an empty state: 'No eligible users. Assign the board_member role in Users & Roles', linking to that tab.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. Add and remove only mutate local rosterMemberIds (AdminClient.tsx:34, 324, 360). There is no dirty indicator or leave guard next to Save/Reset (:332-341). updateCommitteeRoster accepts any length, including 0, and ignores Supabase errors on update/insert (actions.ts:280-315). The 'Unknown User' + raw-UUID fallback is at :317-318. The eligible list has no empty state (:346-365). Note that it also includes founder_admin as eligible, not just board_member, so the proposed empty-state copy should mention both. The 7-person board is documented (docs/credit-issuance/07-approvals-appeals-and-ambiguity-review.md:76, 87), so the quorum guidance rests on the spec. The recommendation is sound.

### #106 [P2] Back-office layouts break on narrow screens and controls lack accessible names and label bindings
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** Several layouts break below desktop width. The Admin header puts a 4-tab TabsList and two link buttons in a non-wrapping justify-between row. Imports uses a fixed 2-column grid, Aliases a fixed 3-column grid, the roster a fixed 2-column grid, and the party table is 8 columns. On a tablet or phone all of these overflow or crush. Icon-only edit, delete and remove buttons have no aria-label, so screen readers announce 'button'. Most <Label>/<label> elements aren't tied to their controls with htmlFor/id: create-user fields, import type, merge selects. Clicking a label doesn't focus the field, and assistive tech reads unlabeled inputs. Navigation to Imports and Aliases uses raw <a>, which forces a full page reload.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:99-115 - non-wrapping header row; <a href> at :108-113`
- `src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:107 - grid-cols-2 without breakpoint`
- `src/app/admin/aliases/AliasesClient.tsx:54 - grid-cols-3 without breakpoint`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:305 - roster grid-cols-2 without breakpoint`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:183-188, 283-285, 320-327 - icon-only buttons with no aria-label`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:209-225 - Labels without htmlFor; native select at :223`
- `src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:114-115 and src/app/admin/aliases/AliasesClient.tsx:62-71 - labels not associated with selects`

**Recommendation.** Use md:/lg: breakpoints: grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2/3, flex-wrap on the admin header, and a horizontally scrollable TabsList. Collapse low-priority party columns (GSTIN, PAN, Industry) below md. Add aria-label to every icon button ('Edit Acme Corp', 'Delete user Jane'). Bind labels with htmlFor/id, or switch to the shadcn Select with Label. Replace <a> with next/link.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. The header is a non-wrapping justify-between row with raw <a href> links (AdminClient.tsx:99-115). Fixed grids with no breakpoints: grid-cols-2 in ImportsClient.tsx:107, grid-cols-3 with col-span-1/2 in AliasesClient.tsx:54, grid-cols-2 in the roster at AdminClient.tsx:305. The party table has 8 columns. The icon-only Pencil/Trash2 buttons at :183-188, :283-285 and :320-327 have no aria-label. The create-user Labels have no htmlFor/id and use a native select (:209-225). The import type and merge select labels are not associated (ImportsClient.tsx:114-115; AliasesClient.tsx:62-71). The CityCodeManager labels are also unbound. RcaReasonManager/DelayReasonManager already use aria-label and are correctly not cited. P2 is appropriate for a desktop-first back office.

### #107 [P2] Opportunity: turn the Admin landing into a 'needs attention' control center with suggested duplicate merges
*Area:* admin-settings · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The admin lands on an alphabetical party table. The real back-office work is exception handling: candidate parties awaiting merge, failed or partial import jobs, grandfathered cases with unmatched RMs, users with no role (self-signups), a committee roster below quorum, and settings without metadata. Today each one is hidden on its own screen, or not visible anywhere, so the founder has to know where to look. Merging is entirely manual even though the data needed to suggest duplicates is already there: is_candidate, legal names, GSTIN and PAN.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:98 - defaultValue='parties' (retrieval list as landing)`
- `src/app/admin/aliases/actions.ts:10 - is_candidate is fetched but only shown as a badge`
- `src/app/admin/imports/actions.ts:316 - rm_matched=false cases never surfaced`
- `src/utils/auth-actions.ts:31 - role-less users resolve to 'viewer' with no admin visibility`

**Recommendation.** Add a top strip of attention cards, each showing a count and a one-click destination: 'Candidate parties (12) → Review', 'Imports with errors (2)', 'Grandfathered cases without RM (40) → Reassign', 'Users without a role (3)', 'Roster 5/7'. On Aliases, compute suggested pairs (same GSTIN or PAN, or normalised-name similarity) and show them as 'Possible duplicate: A ↔ B · Review merge' rows that open the merge preview pre-filled.

*Verification:* confirmed: The evidence holds. The Admin page defaults to the 'parties' tab (AdminClient.tsx:98). is_candidate is read only in aliases (fetched at aliases/actions.ts:10 and shown as a badge). rm_matched is never surfaced anywhere (grep finds it only in imports/actions.ts, and original_rm_name is used only for display in CollectionsClient). Role-less users are invisible to admins. This is an opportunity built on real gaps already covered by findings 3, 6, 8, 9 and 14. Caveat: the 'Grandfathered cases without RM' card must not rely on rm_matched alone, because finding 6 shows rm_matched can be true while rm_user_id is the importer. Compare rm_user_id against import_jobs.imported_by, or fix the import first. The duplicate suggestions (same GSTIN/PAN, normalised name) are feasible with the existing columns.

### #108 [P2] format.ts is bypassed about 70 times: audit times in server timezone, missing amounts shown as ₹0, relative times with no exact value
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* consistency · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, kam, accounts, rm, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** format.ts exists, but only 17 files import it. The rest format inline, and some of that formatting is wrong. The global audit log is a server component calling `new Date().toLocaleString()` with no locale or timezone, so audit timestamps render in the server's timezone (UTC on most hosts) and US format. That is wrong for an IST business audit trail. The case Overview renders `(c.bill_amount || 0)` and exposure `|| 0`, so a missing bill or bureau value displays as a confident '₹0'. The case list shows '0d requested terms' when the value is missing. LedgerTab and CollectionsClient each define their own fmt and formatINR, again with `|| 0` and no IST. format.ts itself uses four different missing-value words ('Not available', '—', 'Not set', 'Unknown'). relativeDays is used 13 times and none of them exposes the exact timestamp. ValueProgression shows the money reconciliation in compact ₹L, which hides small shortfalls between Decided, Promised and Collected. The audit log shows truncated case UUIDs ('Case 1a2b3c4d...') instead of case numbers.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/audit/page.tsx:42 — new Date(e.created_at).toLocaleString() in a server component; :38 — `Case ${e.case_id.slice(0, 8)}...``
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:390; src/app/policy/page.tsx:132 — toLocaleString()/toLocaleDateString() with no locale or IST`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:174-175, :285, :290, :324, :329 — (x || 0).toLocaleString → ₹0 for missing; :310, :349 'Data as of' in default locale`
- `src/app/cases/page.tsx:295 — `{c.composite_credit_days || 0}d requested terms``
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:88-92 — private fmt/fmtDate with no IST timezone`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:70 — private formatINR with (n || 0); :1260, :1276, :1587 inline toLocaleDateString`
- `src/lib/format.ts:13, :19, :32, :64 — four different missing sentinels`
- `src/components/creditflow/TermsLadder.tsx:94 — ValueProgression uses formatCompactINR for the reconciliation`

**Recommendation.** Add an ESLint no-restricted-syntax rule banning .toLocaleString, .toLocaleDateString and .toLocaleTimeString outside src/lib/format.ts. Add formatDays(n) and a `<RelativeTime date>` component that renders `<time dateTime title={formatDateTimeIST}>`. Settle one missing-value vocabulary that follows §13.6. Replace every `|| 0` on displayed money with formatINR(null), which yields 'Not provided'. Use exact formatINR in ValueProgression. Resolve case_id to case_number in the audit queries.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: audit/page.tsx (a server component) calls new Date(...).toLocaleString() with no locale or timezone and shows `Case ${case_id.slice(0,8)}...`. OverviewTab:174-175 and :285/:290 use (x || 0).toLocaleString, so a missing value renders as ₹0. cases/page.tsx:295 renders `{composite_credit_days || 0}d requested terms`. CollectionsClient:70 defines formatINR with (n || 0). format.ts uses four sentinels ('Not available', '—', 'Not set', 'Unknown'). TermsLadder ValueProgression uses formatCompactINR. Corrections: LedgerTab's private fmt returns '—' for null (not || 0), and its fmtDate omits IST but runs client-side, so the browser timezone, typically IST, applies. Bill amount is required at intake, so ₹0-for-missing mostly affects optional exposure fields. P2 holds. The audit-timestamp timezone issue is the most serious part.

### #109 [P2] Icon-only buttons have no accessible name, including the close button on every Dialog
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin, kam, accounts

**Problem.** Around 20 icon-only Pencil, Edit and Trash buttons have neither aria-label nor title. Screen readers announce them as 'button', and sighted users get no tooltip. Delete and edit look identical apart from colour. The shared Dialog close button has no sr-only label and suppresses focus with focus:outline-none, which affects every dialog in the app. Explanatory text is also hover-only. StatusBadge's meaning is available only through a native title attribute, which keyboard and touch users cannot reach. Role revocation depends on a hover colour change. CaseLifecycleActions already shows the correct pattern but nothing enforces it.

**Evidence.**
