- `src/components/ui/dialog.tsx:39-41 — DialogClose with <X/>, no sr-only 'Close', focus:outline-none`
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:183-185, :188, :283-285, :320-327 — Pencil/Trash with no label`
- `src/app/settings/CityCodeManager.tsx:91-93; src/app/policy/grades/GradesClient.tsx:94-96; src/app/policy/personas/PersonasClient.tsx:93-95`
- `src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:106, :109; src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:222, :225, :411`
- `src/app/policy/validity/ValidityClient.tsx:111-117; src/app/policy/routing/RoutingClient.tsx:145-150; src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:114`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:576-578, :582-585 — edit and delete payment icon buttons`
- `src/components/creditflow/StatusBadge.tsx:59 — meaning only via title=`
- `src/components/creditflow/CaseLifecycleActions.tsx:41-42 — the correct pattern (aria-label + title)`

**Recommendation.** Add an `IconButton` primitive whose `label` prop is required by TypeScript. It renders aria-label and a visible tooltip on hover and focus, and new icon buttons should go through it. Codemod every size='icon' or icon-only Button to use it. Add `<span className='sr-only'>Close</span>` and a focus-visible ring to DialogClose. Give StatusBadge an optional popover or aria-describedby for the meaning, or rely on showMeaning in the case header.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. dialog.tsx:39-41 renders DialogClose with a bare <X/>, no sr-only text and focus:outline-none. That utility overrides the base-layer :focus-visible outline, so focus is invisible. The Pencil/Trash icon Buttons in AdminClient (183-185, 283-285, 320-327) and in the policy clients have no aria-label or title. StatusBadge's meaning is available only through title=. CaseLifecycleActions:42 shows the correct aria-label pattern. Downgraded to P2: this is an internal ops tool, the issue blocks no sighted user's core job, and the icons are conventional. The recommendation (an IconButton with a required label) is sound.

### #110 [P2] About 80% of form labels are not tied to their inputs, including money and date fields
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam, accounts, founder_admin

**Problem.** 96 of 119 <Label>/<label> elements have no htmlFor, and 129 of 163 visible inputs, selects and textareas have no id or aria-label. Clicking a label does not focus its field, screen readers announce 'edit text, required' with no name, and no field-level errors are wired up with aria-describedby or aria-invalid. The worst cases are the highest-stakes forms. Party creation (PAN, GSTIN, customer code), the ledger's Promised Amount, Decided Amount, Payment Date and Amount fields, intake's Total Site Value, and the policy parameter editor are all affected. The global search input has only a placeholder.

**Evidence.**
- `src/components/admin/PartyDialog.tsx:101-162 — 12/12 labels have no htmlFor (Legal Name, Customer Code, GSTIN, PAN…)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:289-296, :318-319, :470-484 — Promised, Decided and payment Amount/Date labels not associated`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:555-556 — 'Total Site Value (₹) *' label not associated with its input`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx — 16 of 19 controls unlabelled; src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx — 15/15`
- `src/components/SearchBar.tsx:20-26 — input with placeholder only, no label or aria-label`

**Recommendation.** Build a `<Field label hint error required>` wrapper that uses React useId to set the label's htmlFor and the input's id, aria-describedby (hint and error) and aria-invalid, then codemod the existing forms onto it, starting with LedgerTab, PartyDialog and NewCaseForm. Give SearchBar aria-label='Search cases and parties'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Spot-checked. PartyDialog has 12 <Label>s and 0 htmlFor. LedgerTab has 11 labels and 0 htmlFor; the Promised Amount and payment Amount labels at :293-296 and :470-473 are unassociated. CollectionsClient has 0 htmlFor. NewCaseForm:555-556 'Total Site Value (₹) *' is a bare <label> next to an input with no id. SearchBar has a placeholder only. NewCaseForm's tranche inputs do have aria-labels. The exact totals (96/119) were not recounted but are consistent with the samples. P2 is right.

### #111 [P2] Keyboard focus is removed on tabs, links wrap buttons, there is no skip link, and the mobile drawer doesn't trap focus
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, ordinary_approver, board_member, founder_admin

**Problem.** globals.css adds a good :focus-visible outline, but the shadcn Tabs trigger overrides it with focus-visible:outline-none and puts no ring in its place. On the case workspace, the most-used screen, keyboard users can't see which of the six tabs has focus. Inputs use a 1px ring-1, which is hard to see on a 1px border. Ten places nest <Button> inside <Link> (a button inside an anchor), which is invalid HTML and creates two tab stops per control. There is no skip-to-content link past the sidebar and header. The mobile drawer declares role='dialog' aria-modal but never moves focus into itself, doesn't trap focus, and doesn't return focus to the menu button.

**Evidence.**
- `src/components/ui/tabs.tsx:27 — TabsTrigger 'focus-visible:outline-none' with no ring; :41 TabsContent the same`
- `src/app/globals.css:96-99 — the global focus outline that these classes override`
- `src/components/ui/input.tsx:10, src/components/ui/textarea.tsx:9 — focus-visible:ring-1 only`
- `src/app/cases/page.tsx:203, :214; src/app/cases/[id]/board/BoardClient.tsx:37; src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:607, :1449; src/app/policy/page.tsx:140; src/app/policy/grades/GradesClient.tsx:48; src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:48; src/app/policy/personas/PersonasClient.tsx:44; src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:137 — <Link><Button>`
- `src/components/Shell.tsx:153-172 — drawer with no focus management; :200 <main> with no id or skip link`

**Recommendation.** Give TabsTrigger focus-visible:ring-2 ring-ring ring-offset-2, and raise Input, Select and Textarea to ring-2. Replace the <Link><Button> pairs with <Button asChild><Link/></Button>. Add a visually hidden 'Skip to main content' link targeting <main id='main'>. Rebuild the drawer on Radix Dialog (as a Sheet) to get focus trap and restore.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. tabs.tsx:27 TabsTrigger and :41 TabsContent use focus-visible:outline-none with no ring replacement. input.tsx uses ring-1. cases/page.tsx:203-214 and policy/page.tsx:140 nest <Button> inside <Link>. Shell has no skip link, and <main> has no id. The drawer at Shell.tsx:152-172 is a conditional div with role='dialog' and no focus move, trap or restore; an Escape keydown listener exists at :78. The recommendations (Button asChild, a Radix Sheet) match the shadcn conventions already in use.

### #112 [P2] Admin and policy layouts are desktop-only fixed grids and squash below lg
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* responsive · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The work surfaces (My Work, cases list, intake) are responsive, but the admin, policy and some ledger and collections forms use unprefixed grid-cols-2 and grid-cols-3. Validity, Routing, Dominance and Stages put the edit form in col-span-1 of a fixed three-column grid. On a tablet or a narrow laptop split-screen the form is about a third of the width (about 110-250px), and labels and selects truncate. PartyDialog and the LedgerTab payment form use fixed two-column grids inside a max-w-lg dialog or card, giving roughly 150px inputs on phones for PAN, GSTIN and amount entry. The Collections 'Overdue tranches' table has 6 columns inside an overflow-hidden wrapper in a side drawer, so the right-hand columns (Outstanding, DPD) are clipped on mobile rather than scrolled.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/validity/ValidityClient.tsx:50-51; src/app/policy/routing/RoutingClient.tsx:56; src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:39; src/app/policy/stages/StagesClient.tsx:33 — grid-cols-3 with no breakpoint`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:58, :96; src/app/admin/imports/ImportsClient.tsx:107; src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:305 — grid-cols-2 with no breakpoint`
- `src/components/admin/PartyDialog.tsx:99 — grid-cols-2 inside the dialog`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:468, :526 — grid-cols-2 for amount and date`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1244-1245 — 6-column table inside an overflow-hidden wrapper`

**Recommendation.** Default every form grid to grid-cols-1 with sm:grid-cols-2 or lg:grid-cols-3. For policy editors, stack the form above the table below lg, or move editing into a Sheet. Change the Collections drawer table wrapper to overflow-x-auto, or render a stacked card list below sm. Add a Playwright smoke test at 375px and 768px for /policy/*, /admin and the ledger.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. Validity, Routing, Dominance and Stages use unprefixed grid-cols-3 with the form in col-span-1. PartyDialog:99 uses grid-cols-2. LedgerTab:468 and :526 use grid-cols-2 for payment amount and date. CollectionsClient:1244-1245 puts a 6-column table inside 'overflow-hidden'. The policy and admin parts alone would be P3 (desktop admin work), but the ledger payment form and the clipped Outstanding/DPD columns in the collections drawer affect field KAM work on phones, so P2 holds.

### #113 [P2] SubmitButton pending state is form-wide and generic; many forms have none
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* board_member, ordinary_approver, kam, accounts, founder_admin

**Problem.** SubmitButton reads useFormStatus().pending, which is true for every submit button in the same form. In the Decision-tab ballot, clicking 'Vote reject' turns all three buttons (approve, reject, abstain) into 'Saving...' at once. For a few anxious seconds the voter can't confirm which ballot is being cast. 10 of 11 usages keep the generic 'Saving...' default, even for votes and escalations. Many consequential forms use a plain <Button type='submit'> with no pending state at all: the board-page vote buttons, write-off and credit-note approval, policy deletes and assign role. That invites double submission and leaves no sign anything happened.

**Evidence.**
- `src/components/ui/submit-button.tsx:10-19 — pending applies form-wide; default loadingText 'Saving...'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/ApprovalsTab.tsx:522-524 — three SubmitButtons in one form, all switch to 'Saving...'`
- `grep: 11 <SubmitButton> usages, 10 without loadingText`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/board/BoardClient.tsx:213-221 — plain submit Buttons with no pending or disabled state`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:339, :701 — plain submit Buttons on money approvals`

**Recommendation.** Update SubmitButton to compare useFormStatus().data?.get(props.name) with props.value, so only the clicked button shows a spinner while the others are disabled. Make loadingText a required verb phrase ('Casting reject vote…'). Use SubmitButton, or the proposed ActionForm, for every server-action form, and follow each vote or money action with a role='status' receipt that names what was recorded.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: submit-button.tsx uses form-wide useFormStatus().pending with the default 'Saving...'. 10 of 11 usages have no loadingText. In ApprovalsTab:522-524, all three ballot SubmitButtons switch to 'Saving...' together. The BoardClient vote buttons and the LedgerTab money-approval buttons are plain Buttons with no pending state. Correction: assign role already uses SubmitButton (AdminClient 'Assign'), so it does have a pending state. The proposed fix of comparing useFormStatus().data with the button's name and value is valid React 19 API.

### #114 [P2] Opportunity: deep-link into the exact task or decision and keep the workspace tab in the URL
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* bdo, accounts, kam, rm, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** Every queue row, Kanban card and notification links to the bare /cases/{id}. The workspace tab is an uncontrolled Radix defaultValue chosen by a role and status heuristic, so an RM or KAM who clicks 'Open task' on a Stage 2 task in My Work lands on Summary and has to find the Work tab and then the task. Refresh, Back and sharing a link with a colleague all lose the tab. Approvers clicking 'Review and decide' happen to land correctly only because of the heuristic. Every handoff costs a few seconds and a moment of reorientation.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:321 — <Tabs defaultValue={defaultTab}> not synced to the URL`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:109-115 — defaultTab heuristic; RM/KAM 'In Review' → summary`
- `src/app/page.tsx:432-445 — WorkItemRow 'Open task' href=`/cases/${c.id}` with no tab or task`
- `src/components/creditflow/TaskKanban.tsx:30 — card href=`/cases/${t.case.id}``
- `src/app/page.tsx:677-679 — upcoming collection rows all link to the generic /collections, not to the case drawer`

**Recommendation.** Control the Tabs from a `?tab=` search param (router.replace on change). Accept `?task=<id>` and `?round=<id>` to scroll to and briefly highlight the target. Update WorkItemRow and TaskKanban callers, notification link_url generation, and the collections rows (`/collections?case=<id>` to open its drawer). This also gives shareable links for comments and escalations.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. CaseWorkspace:321 uses <Tabs defaultValue={defaultTab}>, uncontrolled. Case page searchParams read only 'receipt'. The defaultTab heuristic at :113-118 sends RM and KAM in 'In Review' to summary. TaskKanban:30 links to bare /cases/{id}. My Work collections rows link to the generic /collections. This is an opportunity with a sound recommendation; P2 is fine.

### #115 [P2] Opportunity: a shared MoneyInput with Indian grouping and a lakh/crore echo to stop order-of-magnitude errors
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, accounts

**Problem.** There are 38 type='number' inputs, and the ₹ amounts that drive scoring, margin and collections (Total Site Value, Decided Amount, Promised Amount, payment Amount, tranche value) are among them. They show raw ungrouped digits with placeholder '0'. Telling 850000 from 8500000 at a glance is error-prone, and a missed zero on the Decided amount silently moves the margin baseline. Native number inputs also change value on mouse-wheel scroll while focused, which is a known way amounts get corrupted. The pattern also differs from form to form: some parse with `|| 0`, others keep strings.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:556 — Total Site Value: type='number' placeholder='0', parseFloat || 0, no formatted echo`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:295-296 (Promised), :319 (Decided), :471-473 (payment Amount) — type='number' placeholder='0'`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:621 — tranche value with the same pattern`
- `grep: 38 type='number' inputs across src/app; no onWheel guard anywhere`

**Recommendation.** Build `<MoneyInput name value onChange>` with inputMode='decimal' (text input, so no wheel changes), live helper text such as '₹8,50,000 · 8.5 lakh' using formatINR and a words helper, a min/max hint, and an optional 'differs from bill by X%' warning where a reference value exists (Decided vs Bill, Promised vs Decided). Swap it in on intake, the ledger and collections. Pair it with the Field component for label and error wiring.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. Grep finds 37 type='number' inputs in src/app (the finding says 38) and no onWheel guard. NewCaseForm:556 Total Site Value and :621 tranche value use type='number', placeholder '0' and parseFloat || 0. LedgerTab Promised and payment Amount are type='number' with placeholder '0' and no formatted echo. The order-of-magnitude risk on money fields that drive margin and collections is real. P2 is right, and the MoneyInput recommendation fits format.ts's formatINR.

### #116 [P2] Intake 'Continue' is disabled with no reason, and an empty party list gives no way forward
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, founder_admin

**Problem.** Each intake step disables Continue until hidden rules pass. Step 2 alone requires all of: site value > 0, exposure > 0, exposure ≤ site value, tranches reconciled, and within the credit limit. The button turns grey and nothing says which rule failed. The stepper also disables later steps. Live on /cases/new, the customer dropdown held only '-- Select Customer --' and Continue was greyed out, with no hint that the list is empty or that 'Add New' is the next step. A first-time RM gets stuck.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:375-391 — canGoNext rules`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:543, 670, 720 — `disabled={!canGoNext(n)}` with no explanation`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:474-479 — the select has no empty-list option`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:402 — the 'Cases' breadcrumb is a plain span, not a link`
- `Live: /cases/new, founder_admin, 1024px and 375px`

**Recommendation.** Keep Continue enabled. On click, validate and show an error summary that links to each failing field (reuse the missingTaskIds pattern at lines 364-373). Or put a short 'To continue: select a customer' checklist next to the button. When the filtered party list is empty, show 'No customers yet — Add a customer' inline. On mobile, show 'Step 1 of 4' because the horizontal stepper hides steps 3–4.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: canGoNext (l.375-391) and `disabled={!canGoNext(n)}` (543, 670, 720) are confirmed, as is the breadcrumb plain span (l.402). Overstated for step 2: inline reasons already exist there for the credit-limit breach (l.599-601 destructive text), tranche reconciliation (l.662 message, plus red Remaining-to-allocate at 645-652), and exposure > site value (impossible via the 0-100% slider). Steps 1 and 3 do lack reasons. The empty customer dropdown seen live is a bypass artifact (no parties loaded), though an 'Add New' link sits right next to the label. The code gap of no empty-list hint is real but minor. Downgraded to P2.

### #117 [P2] The same row shows different amounts by role, and the requested credit amount has a different name on each screen
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, kam, rm, board_member

**Problem.** The same WorkItemRow 'amount' shows different numbers depending on the queue. The approver queue shows requested_exposure_amount, falling back to bill_amount. RM, KAM, board and recent-activity rows show bill_amount, which is the total site value, not the credit asked for. Approvers and KAMs can compare different numbers without knowing it. The vocabulary also drifts: intake says 'Total Site Value' and 'Expected Credit Exposure'; the cases list says '₹X bill'; the party card says 'Last case site value'. The policy hub says bands map to 'recommended credit days', but the Bands page says 'approved credit day buckets', which mixes up a recommendation with an approval.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/page.tsx:578 — approver: `amount={c?.requested_exposure_amount || c?.bill_amount}``
- `src/app/page.tsx:449, 470, 486, 505, 527, 601, 670 — other queues use `amount={c.bill_amount}``
- `src/app/cases/page.tsx:292 — `{formatCompactINR(c.bill_amount)} bill``
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:556, 561, 494 — 'Total Site Value', 'Expected Credit Exposure', 'Last case site value'`
- `src/app/policy/page.tsx:33 vs src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:51 — 'recommended' vs 'approved'`

**Recommendation.** Pick one name for each quantity and add it to the glossary: Site value, Requested credit, Recommended days, Approved days. Make WorkItemRow take `amountLabel` and show 'Requested credit ₹X' in every queue. On the cases list show 'Site ₹X · Credit ₹Y'. Change the Bands subtitle to 'Map score ranges to recommended credit days'.

*Verification:* confirmed: page.tsx:578 uses requested_exposure_amount || bill_amount for approvers. Lines 450, 471, 486, 505, 528, 542, 557, 601 and 670 use bill_amount (several more lines than cited). cases/page.tsx:292 says '₹X bill'. Intake labels and policy/page.tsx:33 vs BandsClient.tsx:51 'recommended' vs 'approved' are confirmed. Downgraded P1->P2: queue rows are navigation summaries, not where decisions are made, so the inconsistency confuses but is unlikely to mislead a decision directly. Recommendation sound.

### #118 [P2] Policy version context is misleading: 'No version exists' when drafts exist, a silent fallback to live, and a green badge on every version
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** resolvePolicyVersion returns only the active version. If drafts exist but nothing is published (a normal first-setup state), every policy subpage shows 'No policy version exists yet — use + New Draft'. That pushes the admin to create yet another draft, and the link goes back to the hub they just left. If `?v=` points to a deleted or invalid draft, the function silently falls back to the live version, so the admin may edit live policy while believing it is a draft. On Parameters, the version badge is always variant="success" (green) whether the version is live, draft or archived. Add Parameter, Add Band and the routing 'New Rule' form stay enabled when no version exists.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:47-63 — invalid ?v falls through to `.eq('is_active', true)`; drafts are never considered`
- `src/app/policy/PolicyContextBar.tsx:17-21 — 'No policy version exists yet' links to /policy (itself)`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:140-144 — `<Badge variant="success">{activePolicy.version_label}` regardless of status`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:149 — Add Parameter is always enabled`
- `Live: /policy, /policy/parameters, /policy/bands, /policy/routing, /policy/simulation as founder_admin`

**Recommendation.** When `?v` is invalid, show a 'Version not found' error instead of falling back to live. When no version is active but drafts exist, say 'No live policy — 2 drafts' and offer to open the latest draft. Colour the badge by status (success = live, info = draft, muted = archived), matching the context bar. Disable add/edit actions when no version exists, with a tooltip saying why.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: resolvePolicyVersion (actions.ts:47-63) falls back to active when ?v is invalid and never considers drafts. ParametersClient 141-144 shows Badge variant=success unconditionally. Add Parameter (l.149) is always enabled, and assertEditableVersion returns early on a null versionId (l.68). Mitigation the finding misses: every subpage renders PolicyContextBar with the resolved version, so a silent fallback to live shows the amber 'Live — Editing the published policy… Changes apply immediately' banner. The admin is not editing live while believing it is a draft. Remaining harm: 'No version exists' when only drafts exist (with a self-link), plus the misleading green badge. Downgraded to P2.

### #119 [P2] Horizontal overflow on /admin (even at desktop width), /collections and /settings
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* responsive · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin, accounts, rm, kam

**Problem.** Measured live. On /admin at the 1024px desktop viewport, main is 974px of content in a 768px column: 'Go to Data Imports' is clipped and 'Party Merge & Aliases' is off-screen (left edge at 1062px). At 375px, /admin is 673px wide, /collections is 454px (the header 'Add legacy case' button is clipped, and the title is squeezed into a narrow column so the description wraps over 4 lines), and /settings is 456px (tab list). Separately, 'Add legacy case' suggests a single-case form but actually opens the CSV import page.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:98-113 — a non-wrapping `flex justify-between` holds 4 tab triggers plus 2 buttons`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:573-611 — header `flex items-start justify-between` with a `shrink-0` action group`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:607-611 — 'Add legacy case' links to /admin/imports?type=grandfathered_cases`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:60-64 — TabsList cannot scroll`
- `Live measurement: main.scrollWidth vs clientWidth — /admin 974/768 at desktop, 673/375 on mobile; /collections 454/375; /settings 456/375`

**Recommendation.** Let header action rows wrap (`flex-wrap`, or stack below the title under sm). Make TabsList `overflow-x-auto` with scroll-snap, or switch to a select on mobile. Move Admin's 'Data Imports' and 'Party Merge' into the tab bar or an overflow menu. Rename the button 'Import legacy cases (CSV)'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Structural and data-independent. AdminClient.tsx:98-113 is a non-wrapping flex with 4 triggers plus 2 buttons. The desktop sidebar w-64 appears at lg (1024px), leaving a 768px column, which matches the measurement. CollectionsClient 573 header is flex justify-between with a shrink-0 actions group. TabsList (ui/tabs.tsx:14) is inline-flex w-full with no overflow handling. 'Add legacy case' links to /admin/imports?type=grandfathered_cases (title attr says CSV). Recommendation sound.

### #120 [P2] Accessibility: unlabelled fields, links nested inside buttons, and severity colours below contrast minimums on small text
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, founder_admin, accounts

**Problem.** Many intake labels have no htmlFor, so screen readers announce unnamed selects and inputs: Customer Party, Total Site Value, City Code, Site ID, Site Address, Strategic Justification, and every intake question label. The PartyDialog Labels have no htmlFor. The global header search has only a placeholder, with no label and no role=search. The role switcher select is labelled by a <p>. Phase filter chips on /cases and Collections' 'Add legacy case' put a <button> inside an <a>, which creates double tab stops and invalid markup. Small text uses the base severity tokens (text-success ~#22c55e and text-warning ~#f59e0b on white, roughly 2:1) instead of the -strong text variants the codebase convention requires.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:465, 556, 683, 690, 695, 707, 737 — <label> without htmlFor or id pairing`
- `src/components/admin/PartyDialog.tsx:160-178 — <Label> without htmlFor`
- `src/components/SearchBar.tsx:18-26 — no aria-label or role=search`
- `src/components/RoleSwitcher.tsx:38-39 — <p> used as the label for the select`
- `src/app/cases/page.tsx:203-212, 214-223 — <Link><Button> nesting`
- `src/app/cases/page.tsx:284, 294; src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:484, 523, 636, 650, 812 — text-success or text-warning on text-xs`

**Recommendation.** Give every label htmlFor and every control a matching id (the step-4 inputs already have ids `intake-task-*`). Add `role="search"` and `aria-label="Search cases and parties"` to the header form. Use `<Button asChild><Link/></Button>` for the chips. Replace text-success and text-warning on text with -strong variants, and add an ESLint rule banning `text-(success|warning|info|attention)(?!-strong)` on text elements.

*Verification:* confirmed: NewCaseForm labels without htmlFor are confirmed (Customer Party l.465, Total Site Value l.556, City Code, Site ID, Site Address, Strategic Justification, question labels around 683-737). KAM and case-scenario do have htmlFor. PartyDialog Labels lack htmlFor. SearchBar has no aria-label or role=search. RoleSwitcher uses a <p> 'Impersonate Role:' with no association. cases/page.tsx:203-223 nests Link>Button. text-success/text-warning on small text is confirmed (cases 284/294, NewCaseForm 484/523/636). Base tokens are success 45% lightness and warning amber-500, while -strong variants exist in globals.css:44-45 and tailwind.config. Contrast ratios are approximate but clearly below 4.5:1.

### #121 [P2] The Audit & Logs page can't support a real audit question
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** /audit loads a fixed 200 events. The badge reads '200 events', which looks like a total. There are no filters (actor, event type, case, date), no search and no export. A case appears as a truncated UUID ('Case 1a2b3c4d...') that is not a link, and timestamps use toLocaleString in the server's locale, while the rest of the app uses IST helpers. The same log is duplicated as Admin's 'Global Audit Log' tab, so the nav has two paths to one weak view.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/audit/page.tsx:7 — fetchGlobalAuditLog(200)`
- `src/app/audit/page.tsx:20 — `{events.length} events` badge`
- `src/app/audit/page.tsx:38 — `Case ${e.case_id.slice(0, 8)}...`, not linked`
- `src/app/audit/page.tsx:42 — `new Date(e.created_at).toLocaleString()``
- `src/app/admin/AdminClient.tsx:104 — duplicate 'Global Audit Log' tab`

**Recommendation.** Add URL-driven filters (event type, actor, case number, date range), pagination with a real total, CSV export, case_number links to /cases/[id]?tab=audit, and formatDateTimeIST. Remove the Admin tab or make it link to /audit. Show before/after values for policy and settings events, as doctrine Principle 18 asks.

*Verification:* confirmed: audit/page.tsx: fetchGlobalAuditLog(200), a `{events.length} events` badge, `Case ${case_id.slice(0,8)}...` unlinked, `new Date().toLocaleString()` (server locale, not IST). There are no filters or export. AdminClient l.104 duplicates it in the Global Audit Log tab. Recommendation sound.

### #122 [P2] System Settings changes live risk thresholds with one click: no confirmation, no feedback, and a blank tab when empty
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* trust · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The Risk controls tab warns 'These controls affect live business logic immediately', yet each control is a plain form: number input, then Save. There is no confirmation, no before/after preview, no pending state and no success receipt. When no settings load (as live), the tab shows only the warning banner, with no empty or error state. Developer copy is visible to admins: 'No description registered — add it to settingMeta.' and 'Blast radius is not yet registered for this control.'

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:67 — warning banner`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:78-81 — `<form action={updateSystemSetting}>` with a bare Save button, no useFormStatus or confirmation`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:70 — `(settingsRes.data || []).map` gives a silent empty tab`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:76, 82 — developer-facing fallback copy`
- `Live: /settings as founder_admin, Risk controls tab shows only the banner`

**Recommendation.** Confirm before saving with the impact shown: 'Change write-off slippage 5% → 8%. 3 cases pending write-off will be re-evaluated.' Use the blastRadius text that already exists. Show a pending state and a toast receipt with the new value and time. Add an empty or error state. Replace developer copy with 'No description available'.

*Verification:* confirmed: settings/page.tsx:67 banner; l.78-81 has a bare `<form action={updateSystemSetting}>` with a Save button, no confirmation and no pending or receipt state. l.70 `(settingsRes.data || []).map` has no empty or error branch. l.76 and l.82 expose developer fallback copy. meta.blastRadius already exists and could feed a confirmation. The empty tab seen live is a bypass artifact, but the missing empty or error state is a real code gap.

### #123 [P2] My Work order and labels don't fit each role's main job
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, board_member, accounts, bdo, kam

**Problem.** Every role sees 'Tasks assigned to you' first. For an approver the main job, 'Decisions due from you', sits in the right column, and for board members 'Board ballots open' sits second. Accounts, BDO, approvers and admin get a 'Recent activity on your cases' card, but their query is not scoped to them, so the card shows anyone's cases under a 'your' label. Tasks 'View all' goes to /cases, which is not a task list. Upcoming-collection rows link to /collections rather than the case. The role caption in the sidebar is the raw role ('Kam', 'Bdo') instead of ROLE_LABELS. The user's name flips between 'Test RM' and 'Operator' across navigations because fetchSessionInfo is called on the client.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/page.tsx:434-459 — the tasks card always renders first; approver and board queues at 568-610`
- `src/app/page.tsx:255-259, 663 — `scoped()` filters only RM and KAM; title is 'Recent activity on your cases'`
- `src/app/page.tsx:438 — tasks viewAllHref="/cases"`
- `src/app/page.tsx:650 — upcoming rows href="/collections"`
- `src/components/RoleSwitcher.tsx:33-34 — `full_name || 'Operator'`; `capitalize {activeRole.replace('_',' ')}``
- `Live: / as each of the 7 roles — e.g. sidebar shows 'Test RM / Kam'; /policy/parameters showed 'Operator' while other pages showed 'Test RM'`

**Recommendation.** Order queues per role, putting the decision or ballot queue first for approvers and board and the billing queue first for Accounts. Rename the unscoped card 'Recently updated cases'. Point tasks 'View all' to a real task filter (e.g. /cases?assigned=me). Link collection rows to /cases/[id]?tab=ledger. Use ROLE_LABELS in RoleSwitcher. Fetch the session name on the server and pass it into Shell.

*Verification:* confirmed: page.tsx:433-438: the tasks QueueSection always renders first, with viewAllHref='/cases'. Approver and board queues come later. scoped() (l.255-259) only filters RM and KAM, and the card title 'Recent activity on your cases' is unconditional (l.663). Upcoming rows link to /collections (l.650). RoleSwitcher l.34 is `capitalize {activeRole.replace('_',' ')}`, giving 'Kam' and 'Bdo'. The name comes from a client fetchSessionInfo in useEffect, with an 'Operator' fallback until it resolves, which explains the flicker. Recommendation sound.

### #124 [P2] Opportunity: a policy readiness checklist and simulation of a draft against real cases
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The policy hub lists 10 static link cards with no state. The admin can't see which areas are configured, empty or invalid, for example 'Score Bands: 0 bands — required before publish' or 'Weights don't sum to 100 for persona X'. Simulation takes hand-typed customer and contractor scores (0–100) and a dominance dropdown that starts blank with no placeholder. The admin can't answer 'what would this draft have decided on last month's cases, compared with live?'

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/page.tsx:33 (and surrounding card array) — static desc strings, no counts or status`
- `Live: /policy — cards show only titles and descriptions; version history is at the bottom, below the fold`
- `Live: /policy/simulation — three inputs, blank 'Dominance Category' select, Run Simulation enabled`

**Recommendation.** Add a status chip to each hub card (configured count plus a validation state, success/warning/destructive) and a 'Ready to publish' checklist that blocks Publish until required areas pass. Move the version selector and history to the top. Extend Simulation into a 'Draft vs Live' replay: pick N recent cases and show a table of score, band, recommended days and required stage under each version, with the differences highlighted. This turns policy changes from guesswork into evidence.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: The hub cards are static description strings (policy/page.tsx:24-40), and nothing validates or blocks Publish. However, per-version counts (parameters, bands, personas, weights) are already fetched (l.14, fetchPolicyVersionCounts) and shown as an outgoing->incoming diff in the PolicyVersionActions publish confirmation, so the recommendation should reuse them. The 'blank Dominance select' is a bypass artifact: with data, the select has no empty option and defaults to the first category. Simulation does take hand-typed scores, which is confirmed.

### #125 [P2] Cases are flagged ambiguous when critical evidence was waived or can never be graded
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* kam, board_member, founder_admin

**Problem.** checkAmbiguity counts a critical task as missing whenever grade_value is null. It does not exclude waived tasks, so a KAM who properly waives critical evidence still finds the case marked ambiguous and routed to the board. The parameter dialog also lets an admin mark short_text, long_text or date (no band editor) parameters as Critical. Those never get a grade_value, so every case using such a parameter is permanently ambiguous with the opaque reason 'N critical parameter(s) are incomplete', which does not name the parameters.

**Evidence.**
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:281-294 - the query filters only on grade_value IS NULL and has no is_waived/status filter`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:378-379 - elsewhere, waived tasks are rightly excluded via .neq('is_waived', true)`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:296-302 - Critical checkbox is offered for every input type`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:347 - band editor only for numeric/link_list/yes_no; date/text cannot produce grades`

**Recommendation.** Exclude is_waived = true and status = 'Waived' from the missing-critical query, and treat critical text parameters as satisfied when status is Completed. Include the parameter names in the reason ('Missing critical: GST filing, Bank statement'). In the dialog, disable Critical, or warn, when the input type has no grade path.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: checkAmbiguity (scoring.ts:281-294) does filter only on grade_value IS NULL. However, no code anywhere in src sets is_waived=true or status='Waived', so there is no waive action in the app and that half of the finding is latent: it is reachable only through direct DB edits. Confirmed: the Critical checkbox (296-302) is offered for every input type, and short_text/long_text/date can never produce a grade, so such a critical parameter makes every case permanently ambiguous. The reason string uses only the count, even though description/name are already selected. Recommendation is sound, but waiver exclusion is future-proofing, not a live bug.

### #126 [P2] Parameter dialog is still a long flat form with dead Phase 2 fields and no impact feedback
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** Items 3 and 10 are PARTIAL. The dialog is a max-w-lg two-column grid of about 15 fields: name, subject, stage, role, input type, weight, critical, 3 signal fields, SLA, reasoning, stable, rubric and bands. It has no grouping by intent. The signal metadata is honestly labelled 'reserved for Phase 2' but is still rendered and saved. Grade choices are hardcoded 1-5 ('Manual Grade (1-5)', band grade select [1..5], Yes/No default 5/1) instead of using the policy grade scale. 'Date' can be selected but has no band editor, so date parameters score nothing without warning. Changing weight or stage shows no effect on stage capacity, even though the Stages page now computes that.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:237-421 - single flat grid inside max-w-lg`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:303-315 - Phase 2 signal fields still shown`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:335-337 - signal_* still persisted with defaults`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:283,409 - grade range hardcoded to 1-5`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:289,347 - date selectable, but no banding UI for it`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:293-294 - weight input with no capacity context`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:201 - table shows raw input_type keys (e.g. 'grade_select')`

**Recommendation.** Change the dialog to a sheet with four sections: Identity (name, subject, stage, owner), Scoring (input type, weight, bands with a live preview, critical/stable), Evidence & SLA (rubric, reasoning, SLA) and a collapsed Advanced section. Remove the signal fields from the UI until Phase 2. Fill grade options from fetchGradeScales. Add date bands ('days until/since'), or hide date from scored types. Under Weight, show 'Stage 2 customer capacity: 410 / max 400 (+10)' live.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: a max-w-lg flat grid, grades hardcoded 1-5 (lines 283, 352 and 409, plus the Yes/No default of 5/1 at 278), date selectable with no band editor (347), no capacity context for weight, and raw input_type keys in the table (201). Correction: the Phase 2 signal fields are already inside a collapsed <details> labelled 'not yet used in scoring', so they are not 'rendered' by default. They are still persisted (actions.ts:335-337). This is a usability issue with no decision or data risk, so P2 fits better than P1.

### #127 [P2] Dominance editor shows stale values when switching rows and doesn't validate weights
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The form-field relevance part of Item 10 is fixed: only the relevant fields show per method. However, the inputs are uncontrolled (defaultValue) and the form has no key. Clicking Edit on a second category, or Cancel, leaves the previous category's name and weights in the fields, so an admin can save category B with A's weights. After Create the form isn't cleared either. A 'Weighted blend' accepts weights that don't add up to 1, which silently inflates or deflates the final score. Delete is one click with no confirm, and the list columns Cust Wgt/Cont Wgt don't show the exponent.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:46-76 - defaultValue inputs, no key={editingCat?.id}`
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:20-28 - submit doesn't reset the form`
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:62-71 - no sum-to-1 hint or validation`
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:112-117 - immediate delete`

**Recommendation.** Add key={editingCat?.id ?? 'new'} to the form and reset it after a successful submit. For the weighted method, show a live 'Customer 0.6 + Contractor 0.4 = 1.00' check and block saving unless the sum is 1, or state the scaling effect explicitly. Add a delete confirm that lists the scenarios using the category, and show the exponent in the table.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified DominanceClient.tsx: defaultValue inputs and no key on the form. Editing row B after row A, when both use weighted/power_law, leaves A's name and weights in place. The name input never remounts. Weight inputs remount only if the method toggles. Cancel and Create don't reset the form, and Cancel doesn't reset the method. There is no sum check for weighted, delete is immediate (112-117), and the table has no exponent column. Recommendation is sound.

### #128 [P2] Task weight hint ignores persona overrides and uses a misleading unit
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* clarity · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* kam, bdo, accounts

**Problem.** Item 8 is PARTIAL. The new hint 'Weight W — contributes up to W×top pts of this stage's score' uses parameter_definitions.weight, but scoring uses the persona's weight_matrices override when one exists, including 0 for 'excluded'. So reviewers can spend effort on evidence that counts for nothing for this persona. The 'pts' are also raw weighted points, not points on the 0-100 score that approvers see, because stage scores are normalised by max_total. The hint also appears for short_text/long_text parameters that never produce a grade.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:23 - weight = task.param?.weight (default only)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:91-93 - 'contributes up to … pts of this stage's score'`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:43-68 - persona override is the weight actually applied`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:91 - task fetch doesn't bring the persona weight or max_total`

**Recommendation.** In the server data loader, attach effectiveWeight (persona override ?? default) and the stage max_total to each task. Render 'Worth up to 6 of 100 points for this customer (persona: Infra-Tier1)', show 'Not scored for this persona' when the weight is 0, and hide the hint for ungraded input types.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified TaskCompleteForm.tsx:23 uses task.param.weight, and the loader at actions.ts:91 fetches only the default weight, with no persona override or max_total. Scoring applies the weight_matrices override with ?? (0 means excluded). The hint at 91-93 renders for every scoring task, including short_text/long_text/date, and its 'pts' are raw weighted points before max_total normalisation. Recommendation is sound.

### #129 [P2] Dashboard PDCR variants are computed but never shown, and admin approval rate always reads 'Not available'
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* bug · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, founder_admin

**Problem.** Item 12 is OPEN. countPDCR and weightedDaysPDCR are calculated on every dashboard load, but only amountPDCR is shown, so the work is wasted. weightedDaysPDCR is also capped at 100%, which hides late payment: actual/proposed above 100% means late. Separately, the approval-success query always applies .eq('rm_user_id', rmUserId). For admins rmUserId is null, so the filter matches nothing and the card reads 'Not available'. And the early return when a user has no billed cases also nulls approvalSuccessRate, even when there are submitted cases.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/page.tsx:136-146 - countPDCR and weightedDaysPDCR computed; weightedDays capped with Math.min(100, …)`
- `src/app/page.tsx:161 - returned but never rendered`
- `src/app/page.tsx:703-709 - only amountPDCR is shown`
- `src/app/page.tsx:148-152 - unconditional .eq('rm_user_id', rmUserId), null for admins (called at :363 with null)`
- `src/app/page.tsx:32-34 - early return discards approvalSuccessRate`

**Recommendation.** Fix the approval query so it filters on rm_user_id only when rmUserId is set, and compute it before the early return. Either remove the unused PDCR variants or put them in the PDCR card's definition popover as 'On-time by count: X% · Avg days vs promised: Y%'. Remove the 100 cap on days-ratio and present it as '+N days late on average'. Count only resolved cases in the approval rate (exclude In Review).

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified page.tsx:136-146: countPDCR and weightedDaysPDCR are computed, and weightedDays is capped by Math.min(100). Only amountPDCR is rendered (704-708). Line 151 has an unconditional .eq('rm_user_id', rmUserId), which is called with null for admins at :363, so the card yields no rows or an error and shows 'Not available'. The early return at :32-34 nulls approvalSuccessRate whenever there are no billed cases. Recommendation is sound.

### #130 [P2] Opportunity: policy readiness check and impact replay before publishing
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* founder_admin, board_member

**Problem.** The checks that keep a policy coherent are scattered or missing: stage drift on one page, Yes/No mapping enforced only on save, nothing for critical parameters with no grade path, personas without overrides, overlapping routing rules, validity rules with no catch-all, or dominance weights that don't sum to 1. Nothing shows how a draft would have changed recent decisions. The doctrine asks for continuous completeness checks and impact shown at publish time.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/stages/StagesClient.tsx:82-87 - the only conflict check, and only on one page`
- `src/app/policy/actions.ts:200-222 - publishDraftPolicy flips the version state`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:18-47 - no replay of real cases`
- `src/utils/validity.ts:25 - returns null when no rule matches, so the case gets no expiry`

**Recommendation.** Add a 'Readiness' panel to PolicyContextBar for drafts. It should run checks for capacity drift per subject, ungradeable critical params, missing Yes/No mappings, routing conflicts, a missing default validity rule, and dominance weight sums. Each check gets a status chip and a deep link to fix it. Gate Publish on blockers, and show a diff of the last N decided cases (score, band, days, required stage) between the active policy and the draft.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified: publishDraftPolicy (actions.ts:200-222) simply flips the flags with no checks. The only coherence check is the drift note on the Stages page. selectValidityRule returns null when no rule matches (validity.ts:25). Simulation has no case replay. The Yes/No mapping check runs only on parameter save. A readiness panel with publish gating is a sound recommendation that reuses fixes from other findings.

### #131 [P3] Opportunity: Cmd/Ctrl+K quick-jump with recent cases and type-ahead
*Area:* shell-home-nav · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, rm, accounts, founder_admin, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** Finding a known case, the most frequent retrieval job for KAM, RM and Accounts (for example a customer calls about 'CF-2026-0142'), means clicking search, typing, pressing Enter, waiting for a full results page, then clicking again. There is no keyboard shortcut, no type-ahead, no recently viewed cases, and no jump straight to an exact case number match.

**Evidence.**
- `src/components/SearchBar.tsx:10-15 — submit-only, no suggestions`
- `src/components/Shell.tsx:187 — search is the only header affordance; no shortcut registration`
- `src/app/search/page.tsx:17-26 — no recents or empty-query help beyond static text`

**Recommendation.** Add a command palette (shadcn Command) opened with ⌘K or '/'. It shows the last 8 viewed cases (from localStorage or a recent_views table), debounced type-ahead over case number, party and code with status chips, an exact case number match that opens the case directly, and role actions ('New case', 'Open collections', 'My decisions'). This saves several clicks per lookup across hundreds of lookups a week.

*Verification:* confirmed: SearchBar is submit-only, with no shortcut, type-ahead or recents, and the /search empty state is static text. This is a valid opportunity, not a defect. It is a nice-to-have relative to the correctness issues, so lowered to P3.

### #132 [P3] Opportunity: stream My Work section by section instead of one blocking waterfall
*Area:* shell-home-nav · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm, kam

**Problem.** MyWorkPage awaits about 8 query groups one after another: shared, then role queues, approver, board, collections, metrics, admin. For founder_admin, computeRmPortfolioMetrics pulls every billed or closed case and every repayment on every home load. The whole page sits behind the root spinner until the slowest query, usually metrics, finishes, although metrics are the least urgent content on the page.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/page.tsx:294, 311, 322, 333, 343, 356, 363, 367 — sequential awaits`
- `src/app/page.tsx:21-49 — full case and repayment scan for portfolio metrics (unscoped for admin)`
- `src/app/loading.tsx:3-11 — single blocking spinner`

**Recommendation.** Split each QueueSection and the metrics block into async server components wrapped in <Suspense> with row skeletons, so urgent queues paint first and metrics stream in last. Move the PDCR, margin and exposure calculation into a Postgres view or nightly materialized table with an 'as of' timestamp, and show that freshness on the cards (Principle 12).

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Sequential awaits confirmed, and computeRmPortfolioMetrics for admin scans all billed and closed cases plus their repayments, then runs a third sequential query. Overstated: the role blocks are mutually exclusive by the active role, so a given role runs about 3-5 serial groups, not 8 (admin: shared, collections, metrics x3, admin). The Suspense streaming and materialized-view recommendation is sound but is a performance opportunity, so P3.

### #133 [P3] Approvers and board have no Governance view or decision history, and every tab has the same title
*Area:* shell-home-nav · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* ordinary_approver, board_member, founder_admin

**Problem.** For approvers and board members the nav is only 'My Work' and 'Cases'. There is no Governance destination (doctrine 11.1) holding their open decisions, decisions they have already cast, and outcomes, so 'what did I approve last month?' means scrolling an unscoped case list. The root metadata title is static ('CreditFlow — Credit Issuance System') and no page overrides it, so users working five case tabs side by side cannot tell them apart.

**Evidence.**
- `src/components/Shell.tsx:46-65 — no Governance section; approver and board see Work: My Work, Cases`
- `src/app/layout.tsx:8-11 — only metadata definition in the app (no generateMetadata anywhere under src/app)`

**Recommendation.** Add a 'Decisions' nav item for ordinary_approver, board_member and founder_admin, linking to a queue with Due / Cast by me / Outcomes tabs, backed by approval_decisions and board_votes. Add generateMetadata on /cases/[id] ('CF-0142 · Acme · Awaiting decision'), /cases, /collections and the home page ('My Work (3 due)').

*Verification:* confirmed: Shell NAV: approver and board see only My Work and Cases. The only metadata in src/app is the static export in layout.tsx:8-11, with no generateMetadata anywhere. The Decisions view and per-page titles are a valid low-severity opportunity.

### #134 [P3] Vocabulary drifts from the canonical glossary, and 'justification' is only a dropdown
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* copy · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** Intake uses 'Total Site Value', 'Expected Credit Exposure' and 'Last case site value', but the glossary and the workspace say 'Bill amount' and 'Requested exposure'. The RM and approvers therefore see different names for the same number, which is exactly the requested versus recommended confusion the doctrine warns about. The contractor field is labelled 'Contractor / Influencer Party' while its placeholder says 'Select Influencer'. Step 3 promises to capture 'why credit is justified', but justification is a single enumerated reason with no free-text context for the KAM.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:556 - 'Total Site Value (₹)'`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:561, 639 - 'Expected Credit Exposure'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/OverviewTab.tsx:175 - workspace says 'Requested Exposure'`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/AuditTab.tsx:21 - 'Requested exposure'`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:494 - 'Last case site value'`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:504, 514 - 'Contractor / Influencer' vs '-- Select Influencer --'`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:679, 707-713 - 'why credit is justified' served by an enum only`
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:82 - stored as 'Strategic Justification: X' in commercial_notes`

**Recommendation.** Rename to 'Bill amount' and 'Requested exposure (% of bill)' everywhere in intake, and 'Requested terms' for the tranche schedule. Pick one party term and use it in the label, placeholder and review row. Keep the reason enum, and add an optional 'Context for the KAM' textarea (max ~500 chars) stored separately from commercial_notes.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: The label evidence is accurate: 556 'Total Site Value', 561/639 'Expected Credit Exposure', OverviewTab:175 'Requested Exposure', AuditTab:21 'Requested exposure', 494 'Last case site value', 504/514 'Contractor / Influencer' vs 'Select Influencer', the 679 subtitle, the 707-713 enum, and actions.ts:82 'Strategic Justification:'. The framing is overstated. Both names refer to the same requested amount, so this is not the requested-versus-recommended confusion the doctrine warns about, only naming drift. The justification-as-enum point is a product choice, not a defect. Downgraded to P3. The rename recommendation is sound and cheap.

### #135 [P3] Opportunity: live decision rail showing indicative score, headroom, routing and terms as the RM types
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* rm, kam

**Problem.** All the inputs for a meaningful pre-submit preview are already on the client: grade_scale, routing thresholds, the party's credit line and outstanding exposure, last-case composite days, and graded intake answers. Yet the RM sees none of it combined. Today the RM learns whether the ask is realistic only when the KAM counter-offers or returns the case, which costs a full review loop per misjudged ask.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/page.tsx:5-12 - grade scale and routing thresholds already loaded`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:483-495 - credit limit, outstanding and last case shown in separate static cards`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:393-396 - routing evaluated but reduced to a stage number`
- `src/utils/scoring.ts:330 - scoring engine exists server-side and could run on draft answers`

**Recommendation.** Add a sticky right rail on desktop (lg:grid-cols-[220px_1fr_300px]) that collapses into a bottom summary bar on mobile. It would show: (1) credit headroom = limit - outstanding - requested exposure, with a meter; (2) review path in plain language with its reason; (3) an indicative score and band from answered intake parameters, via a new previewIntakeScore server action reusing scoring.ts, labelled 'indicative, based on n of m parameters'; (4) requested composite days vs the party's last case and the policy's max days. Keep sensitive internals at the band level for the RM.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: As an opportunity this is valid: page.tsx:5-12 loads gradeScale and routingThresholds, party cards are static, and routing is reduced to a stage number. However, scoring.ts:330 is updateCycleScore, which reads a persisted review cycle. calculateFinalCaseScore also requires a reviewCycleId, so 'reuse on draft answers' needs a new pure scoring path; the claim is overstated. Exposing an indicative score to the RM also carries a governance risk: RMs could tune answers to a target band. Policy should sign off before this is built. Downgraded to P3 because this is an enhancement, not a defect. The headroom meter and plain-language routing are low-risk subsets worth doing first.

### #136 [P3] Opportunity: autosave, smart defaults and a faster tranche builder to bring intake under two minutes
*Area:* intake · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* rm, kam

**Problem.** Every intake starts from zero, even though the system knows a lot. There is no autosave or last-saved indicator, and the beforeunload guard covers only browser unload, not in-app sidebar navigation, and ignores tranche, KAM and answer edits. The KAM must be picked from all KAMs, though the party's last case or the RM's team implies a default. 'Add Tranche' appends an amount tranche of ₹0 at 0 days, which immediately breaks reconciliation, so the RM must edit two rows. lastCase is fetched but not offered as a template. There are no due dates, presets or 'clone previous case'.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:51-61, 175-177 - beforeunload only; hasContent ignores tranches, KAM and answers`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:222 - addTranche default { type: 'amount', value: 0, days_after_billing: 0 }`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:165-167 - single 100% / 30-day default regardless of party history`
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:166-173 - lastCase (bill, exposure, composite days) fetched but used only as text`
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:699-703 - KAM select has no default`
- `src/app/cases/new/actions.ts:198-207 - fetchKams loads every profile and filters client-side; no team or party affinity`

**Recommendation.** (1) After a party is chosen, autosave the draft (debounced, using the resume path from the first finding) and show 'Saved 12:41' near the actions. Guard in-app navigation as well. (2) Default the KAM to the party's last-case KAM, then the RM's usual KAM, shown as 'Suggested' and editable. (3) The tranche builder gets presets ('100% @ 30d', '50/50 @ 30/60d', 'Same as last case for this party'), 'Add tranche' pre-filled with the remaining unallocated balance at last days + 30, and an estimated due date per row. (4) Add a 'Start from previous case' action that clones scenario, parties, site and terms into a new draft. (5) Add an optional @mention handoff note to the KAM, reusing MentionInput once it is accessible.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Mostly accurate. hasContentRef (175-177) ignores tranches, KAM, answers and cityCode, and the guard is beforeunload-only, so it does not catch in-app navigation. addTranche defaults to amount 0 / 0 days (222). The 100%/30-day default ignores history (165-167). lastCase is text-only, and the KAM select has no default (700-703). One correction: fetchKams filters in the server action, not 'client-side', though it does load every profile. Autosave depends on the resume path from finding 0. Downgraded to P3 as an opportunity. Defaulting the tranche to the remaining balance and adding presets are cheap, sound wins.

### #137 [P3] Opportunity: show exposure against approved credit terms and party limits where collections decisions are made
*Area:* money · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* L · *Personas:* kam, rm, founder_admin

**Problem.** The party roll-up shows only billed and outstanding totals. Neither Collections nor the Ledger shows the approved credit days versus days actually taken, the party's pre-approved credit-days band, or the imported external exposure (party_exposure: outstanding, overdue, overdue_days, data_as_of). The Ledger tranche view says 'Delayed' with no terms context at all. KAMs cannot see at a glance that a contractor approved for 45 days habitually pays in 80, or that they hold ₹30L elsewhere, which is the signal for holding new credit.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1283-1323 — roll-up: billed/outstanding/overdue count only`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1274-1279 — credit terms shown only as a footnote under the tranche table`
- `src/utils/preapproval.ts:34 — getPreapprovedBand exists, unused here`
- `supabase/migrations/20260514000000_remote_schema.sql:628-636 — party_exposure with data_as_of`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:395-433 — tranche waterfall shows no terms or DPD`

**Recommendation.** Add a party strip to the expanded Collections row and the top of the Ledger: 'Approved 45d · Avg days-to-pay 72d (last 5 cases) · Outstanding ₹18.4L across 4 cases · External overdue ₹3.1L (as of 02 Nov)'. Add an attention flag 'Consider holding new credit' when DPD exceeds the band or the party has more than one 60+ case, linking to new case intake and the party page.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Collections roll-up (1283-1323) shows billed, outstanding and overdue count only, and credit terms appear only as a footnote (1274-1279). getPreapprovedBand is not used in collections or the ledger, and the Ledger tranche view has no terms or DPD. Correction: party_exposure (outstanding, overdue, overdue_days, data_as_of) is already fetched for the case (cases/[id]/actions.ts 38, 42) and shown in ApprovalsTab (184-212), so it is one tab away from the Ledger. Avg days-to-pay is not computed anywhere and would be new derived data. This is an opportunity and the data is partly surfaced already, so it is downgraded to P3.

### #138 [P3] Money and date formatting is inconsistent, and exact figures sit behind hover-only tooltips
*Area:* money · *Kind:* consistency · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* accounts, kam, rm

**Problem.** Collection rows show only compact values ('₹1.2L'), and the exact rupee figure is in a title tooltip that touch and keyboard users cannot reach. §13.2 requires exact values for decisions. The compact formatter mixes lowercase 'k' with 'L'/'Cr'. PTP and broken-PTP dates use day-month with no year, which is ambiguous across the new year. The Ledger prints raw toLocaleString timestamps in the payment log, Ledger amounts are not tabular or right-aligned, and 'Delayed' tranches show no day count while Collections shows DPD for the same tranche.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:1200-1209 — compact value, exact only in title`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:71-76 — '₹1.2k' vs 'L'/'Cr'`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:169-170 — shortDate omits year (used at 1170, 1417, 1423)`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:566-569 — raw toLocaleString('en-IN') timestamp`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:241,554 — no tabular-nums`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/LedgerTab.tsx:410-416 — 'Delayed' badge without DPD`

**Recommendation.** Create shared formatINR/formatINRCompact/formatDate helpers in src/lib (IST, 'dd MMM yyyy', with year when it differs from the current year; 'K' capitalised). In rows, show the compact value with the exact value as a visible second line in the expanded panel, and as an accessible label. Add a 'Delayed · 23d' badge and an outstanding column to the Ledger tranche view.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: The row shows the compact value with the exact figure only in a title attribute (1201-1209). The formatter uses lowercase 'k' (74). shortDate has no year (169-170) and is used for PTP and broken-PTP chips. The Ledger payment log uses raw toLocaleString (568). There is no tabular-nums at 241 or 554, and the 'Delayed' badge has no day count (413-415). Correction: the expanded panel already shows exact per-tranche outstanding (1261-1263), the non-payer block shows exact case outstanding (1399), and the LogDrawer header shows exact outstanding. Exact values are therefore reachable without hover, though total outstanding is not shown exactly in the expanded panel for payer roles. These are consistency and polish issues, so P3.

### #139 [P3] Copy, semantics and accessibility gaps: internal doc numbers, 'approved' days, position-based severity colors, unlabeled icon buttons
*Area:* policy · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** Page subtitles cite internal docs ('Doc 06', 'Doc 09'). Band outputs are labeled 'Approved Credit Days' throughout, although bands produce a recommendation, and that blurs recommended and approved. Band colors cycle through severity tokens by position, so the 4th band is always 'destructive' and the 5th 'brand' whatever their risk, and the 'Color' column adds no information. Icon-only Edit and Delete buttons have no aria-label. Most dialog Labels are not linked to inputs (no htmlFor/id). Sortable headers are clickable <th> elements with no keyboard or ARIA sort support. Abbreviations like 'Cust Wgt' and 'Cont Wgt' slow scanning.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/routing/RoutingClient.tsx:48 — '(Doc 06)'`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:54 — '(Doc 09)'`
- `src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:51,140 — 'approved credit day' copy`
- `src/app/policy/bands/BandsClient.tsx:24-33,86,101-103 — position-cycled severity tokens and a decorative Color column`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:222,225; bands/BandsClient.tsx:106,109; grades/GradesClient.tsx:94; dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:109-116 — icon buttons without aria-label`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:246-247,257-258 — Label without htmlFor`
- `src/app/policy/parameters/ParametersClient.tsx:176-188 — onClick on TableHead`
- `src/app/policy/dominance/DominanceClient.tsx:95-96 — 'Cust Wgt'/'Cont Wgt'`

**Recommendation.** Remove the doc references. Rename to 'Recommended credit days' everywhere in policy and Simulation. Color bands on a single sequential ramp ordered by score (or neutral), and drop the Color column. Add aria-labels such as 'Edit Bank statement' and 'Delete band B'. Pair every Label and input with htmlFor/id. Make sort headers <button>s with aria-sort. Spell out 'Customer weight' and 'Contractor weight'.

*Verification:* confirmed: Confirmed: '(Doc 06)' at RoutingClient:48, '(Doc 09)' at SimulationClient:54, and 'approved credit day buckets' at BandsClient:51 plus the 'Approved Credit Days' label. Band colors cycle by position through severity tokens (BandsClient:23-33), and the Color column is aria-hidden decoration. Icon buttons have no aria-label. Most Labels have no htmlFor; some do (is_critical, require_reasoning, is_stable). Sortable TableHead elements use onClick only. 'Cust Wgt' and 'Cont Wgt' appear as stated. The DB column stays approved_credit_days, so rename the copy only.

### #140 [P3] Loading and error states: full-screen spinner on most routes, skeletons that don't match the page
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* usability · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam, accounts, ordinary_approver, founder_admin

**Problem.** Only /admin and /cases/[id] have route-level skeletons. My Work, Cases, Collections, Search, Audit, Settings and all 11 policy pages fall back to the root loading.tsx, a centred spinner reading 'Loading...' that clears the page on every navigation. The case skeleton uses fixed grid-cols-2 and grid-cols-4 and shows 4 tabs, while the real page is single-column on mobile with 6 tabs, so the layout visibly jumps. Inside the workspace, tab Suspense fallbacks are bare 'Loading summary…' text, and the Decision-tab count badge uses fallback={null}, so it pops in late. There is no not-found.tsx, so a mistyped or deleted case URL either hits the generic error boundary or Next's default 404 outside the product's look. Error copy is generic, doesn't say whether data changed, and shows no digest a user could quote to support.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/loading.tsx:5-9 — full-area spinner used by all routes without their own loading file`
- `find src/app -name loading.tsx → only src/app/loading.tsx, src/app/admin/loading.tsx, src/app/cases/[id]/loading.tsx`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/loading.tsx:22-27 (4 tab placeholders vs 6 real tabs), :29, :65 — non-responsive grid-cols-2/grid-cols-4`
- `src/app/admin/loading.tsx:36, :42 — fixed 6-column grid`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/CaseWorkspace.tsx:327, :337, :354, :360 — Suspense fallbacks are null or plain text`
- `src/app/error.tsx:23-26 and src/app/cases/[id]/error.tsx:22-25 — generic copy, no digest, no 'nothing was changed'`

**Recommendation.** Add loading.tsx skeletons for /, /cases, /collections and /policy that reuse the real layout primitives (QueueSection, WorkItemRow and Card skeleton variants), and make them responsive. Give the tab Suspense fallbacks skeletons sized like their panels. Add app/not-found.tsx and cases/[id]/not-found.tsx (calling notFound() when a case is missing) with 'Search cases' and 'Go to My Work' actions. Error pages should say what failed, that no data was changed, show the error.digest as a reference, and offer Retry plus a route back. Root error.tsx should not use h-screen inside the shell.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: only 3 loading.tsx files exist (root, admin, cases/[id]). The case skeleton shows 4 tab placeholders against 8 TabsTrigger elements in CaseWorkspace, and uses fixed grid-cols-2 and grid-cols-4. The tab Suspense fallbacks are null or plain text. There is no not-found.tsx, and error pages show no digest. Overstated: the root loading.tsx is an h-[50vh] spinner inside the shell's main area, not a full-screen wipe. Overstated: cases/[id]/page.tsx:23-28 already calls notFound() for missing or unauthorized cases, so that part of the recommendation is already done; what is missing is only a branded not-found page. Severity lowered to P3, since this is polish rather than lost work.

### #141 [P3] 10-11px text is spreading, including on status and orientation signals
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, ordinary_approver, board_member, accounts

**Problem.** The type scale has split apart. There are 68 uses of text-tiny (10px), 27 of text-[11px] (all in CollectionsClient), 8 of text-[10px] and 1 of text-[9px]. These sizes carry decision signals, not footnotes. MacroProgressRail labels, the main lifecycle orientation, are 10px. WorkItemRow SLA chips ('Overdue 3d') and owner are 10px. TaskKanban due and waiting lines, TermsLadder provenance and the 'current' marker, and EmptyState's failure message are 10px too. Several are also muted with opacity (text-muted-foreground/80), which pushes contrast under AA. For people working several hours a day on laptops this costs reading accuracy. It is the reverse of 'dense where useful'.

**Evidence.**
- `tailwind.config.js:76-78 — fontSize.tiny = 10px/14px is the smallest token`
- `src/components/creditflow/MacroProgressRail.tsx:47, :61, :68 — w-[18px] text-[10px] node labels`
- `src/components/creditflow/WorkItemRow.tsx:52-63 — owner, due chip and meta all text-tiny`
- `src/components/creditflow/TermsLadder.tsx:28, :45, :51, :93 — text-tiny headings, provenance and 'current'`
- `src/components/creditflow/MetricCard.tsx:48 — text-tiny text-muted-foreground/80 scope`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:814-1627 — 27× text-[11px] including DPD bucket pills (987, 1165, 1265, 1309)`
- `src/components/NotificationDropdown.tsx:79 — text-[9px] unread count`

**Recommendation.** Make 12px the minimum size for status, SLA, owner and money text, and allow 11px (a new `text-2xs` token, 11/16) only for non-essential metadata. Remove `tiny` and the arbitrary px sizes through a codemod, and ban opacity modifiers on muted-foreground text. Raise MacroProgressRail labels to text-xs with 20px nodes.

*Verification:* confirmed: Counts verified exactly: 68 text-tiny, 27 text-[11px], 8 text-[10px], 1 text-[9px]. MacroProgressRail uses 18px nodes with text-[10px] labels. The WorkItemRow owner, due chip and meta are text-tiny, and the due chip also uses the low-contrast base warning shade (overlaps finding 1). Real, but this is readability polish and a design-system decision, and much of the damage comes from finding 1's contrast problem. P3 rather than P2.

### #142 [P3] Decorative animation kit: 7 of 8 components unused; the one in use ignores reduced-motion; dialog animation classes do nothing
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* efficiency · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* rm, kam, founder_admin

**Problem.** src/components/animations holds BlurText, CountUp, ShinyText, SpotlightCard, StaggeredFade, StarBorder and TiltedCard. None of them is imported anywhere. Only Squares is used, as an infinitely looping background on the login page. These components conflict with the ops-tool doctrine: CountUp makes metrics count up on every visit, TiltedCard and Spotlight follow the mouse, and ShinyText and StarBorder loop forever. Keeping them invites someone to bring them back. framer-motion stays a production dependency for one background loop. Its animations are JS-driven, so the CSS prefers-reduced-motion guard in globals.css doesn't stop them; only StaggeredFade calls useReducedMotion. Meanwhile dialog.tsx and select.tsx reference animate-in, fade-in-0 and zoom-in-95, but no tailwindcss-animate or tw-animate-css plugin is installed, so those classes do nothing.

**Evidence.**
- `grep 'components/animations' → only src/app/login/page.tsx:12 (Squares)`
- `src/components/animations/Squares.tsx:13-20 — repeat: Infinity background animation; src/components/animations/ShinyText.tsx:25-26, StarBorder.tsx:22-23 — infinite loops`
- `src/components/animations/CountUp.tsx:16-25 — animates numbers from 0 on every mount`
- `src/app/globals.css:103-110 — CSS-only reduced-motion guard (doesn't affect framer-motion)`
- `src/components/ui/dialog.tsx:18, :30; src/components/ui/select.tsx:40 — animate-in classes; package.json has no animate plugin (tailwind.config.js:84 plugins: typography only)`

**Recommendation.** Delete the unused animation components. Make the login background static (or a CSS-only pattern) and drop framer-motion if nothing else needs it. If any motion remains, wrap the app in <MotionConfig reducedMotion='user'>. Either install tw-animate-css for a restrained 150ms dialog fade or remove the dead animate-* classes. Document in the design system that motion is only for explaining state changes, such as a row moving between columns or a receipt appearing.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. Of the 8 animation files, only login/page.tsx imports from components/animations (Squares), and framer-motion is used only there. Squares loops with repeat: Infinity. Only StaggeredFade calls useReducedMotion. The globals.css reduced-motion guard targets CSS animation and transition durations only; framer-motion's JS-driven animation ignores it. dialog.tsx uses animate-in, fade-in-0 and zoom-in-95, but the only Tailwind plugin is typography and no animate package is installed, so those classes are dead. P3 is right.

### #143 [P3] Opportunity: keyboard-first command palette and exact-match case jump
*Area:* design-system · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* kam, rm, accounts, founder_admin, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** Power users here (KAM, Accounts, Admin) touch dozens of cases a day and know them by case number or party. Global search is an unlabelled input with no shortcut. Every lookup is click, type, wait for a results page, then click again, even when the query is an exact case number. There is no way to reach a recent case or run a common action ('record payment', 'open collections for X') from the keyboard.

**Evidence.**
- `src/components/SearchBar.tsx:10-27 — plain input that always pushes /search?q=, with no shortcut, recents or exact-match jump`
- `src/app/search/page.tsx — results page is the only path`

**Recommendation.** Add a Cmd/Ctrl-K palette (and '/' to focus search) built on cmdk or Radix Dialog. It would offer an exact case-number match that opens the case directly, the 5 most recently opened cases, party matches that open the party profile, and role-scoped quick actions: 'New case' for RM; 'Record payment on…' and 'Open collections queue' for KAM/Accounts; 'Pending write-offs' for admin. Keep /search as the 'see all results' fallback.

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified. SearchBar always pushes /search?q= and has no shortcut, recents, label or exact-match redirect. The search page has no redirect for an exact case_number match. No cmdk or global keyboard shortcut exists. This is a legitimate P3 opportunity.

### #144 [P3] Internal jargon and misleading micro-signals leak into the UI
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* copy · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm, accounts

**Problem.** Spec references and roadmap notes appear in the UI: 'Define rules to automatically route cases to deeper stages (Doc 06).', '(Doc 09)', 'richer case-file simulation remains planned'. The Cases error says 'Database error: TypeError: fetch failed' while the header above it says '0 cases'. The access-denied page offers 'Return to Dashboard', but home is called 'My Work', and it doesn't say which role is needed. The routing info note is styled in success green although it is neutral information. The 90+ DPD collections tile is always destructive red, even at ₹0. Recovered is always green.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/routing/RoutingClient.tsx:48 — '(Doc 06)'; :52 — info text in border-success/bg-success`
- `src/app/policy/simulation/SimulationClient.tsx:54 — '(Doc 09)'`
- `src/app/cases/page.tsx:155 and 232 — '0 cases' header together with the raw error message`
- `src/app/unauthorized.tsx:14-18 — 'Return to Dashboard'`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:634 — `tone="severe"` unconditionally; :669 `tone="success"` unconditionally`
