**Recommendation.** Remove Doc references and roadmap notes, or move them behind an info tooltip linking to docs. Hide the case count when the query errored, and replace the raw message with 'We couldn't reach the database. Retry or contact support (ref …)'. Rename the button 'Back to My Work' and say which role grants access. Use info tokens for neutral notes. Make the DPD and Recovered tones depend on their values (neutral at 0).

*Verification:* confirmed: RoutingClient 48 '(Doc 06)' and l.52 neutral info in success tokens. SimulationClient 54 '(Doc 09)'. policy/page.tsx:38 says 'richer case-file simulation remains planned'. cases/page.tsx shows the `{total} cases` header alongside the raw 'Database error: {message}'. unauthorized.tsx says 'Return to Dashboard'. CollectionsClient tiles: 90+ DPD has tone='severe' unconditionally and Recovered has tone='success' unconditionally, while sibling tiles are already value-conditional.

### #145 [P3] The OS dark-mode setting is ignored; the app is light-only
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* consistency · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin, rm, kam, accounts, bdo, ordinary_approver, board_member

**Problem.** With colorScheme=dark emulated, prefers-color-scheme matched (true), but /collections and / rendered unchanged in light (body rgb(248,250,252)). Tailwind is set to darkMode:'class', but no `.dark` token block exists and nothing ever sets the class; there is only one `dark:` usage in src. The globals.css comment shows the team already hit white-on-white select problems under OS dark mode. Users who work late collections shifts get a bright screen. This is low priority for an internal tool, but the semantic tokens already make it cheap.

**Evidence.**
- `tailwind.config.js:3 — darkMode: 'class'`
- `src/app/globals.css:5-50 — only :root tokens, no .dark overrides`
- `src/app/globals.css:115-126 — workaround for OS-dark select rendering`
- `Live: /collections and / with colorScheme dark at desktop — unchanged light rendering`

**Recommendation.** Add a `.dark` token block (including the -strong variants, recalculated for contrast on dark surfaces) and a theme toggle in the user menu that defaults to the system setting. Use next-themes or a small script that sets the class before paint.

*Verification:* confirmed: tailwind.config.js darkMode:'class'. globals.css has no .dark block or prefers-color-scheme rule. There is exactly 1 `dark:` usage in src. globals.css:115-126 is a workaround that forces form-control colours. The app is light-only by construction. It is fair as a low-priority opportunity, not a defect.

### #146 [P3] Opportunity: role-specific first-run experience and collapsed empty queues on My Work
*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* bdo, accounts, ordinary_approver, board_member, rm

**Problem.** When a user has no work, My Work is a grid of identical empty cards: 4 to 6 for RM and KAM, 2 for BDO. On a 375px phone the first screen holds four empty cards and nothing actionable. A new BDO sees 'No open tasks assigned to you.' and 'No cases yet.' with no explanation of what will arrive, from whom, or how they'll be notified. Every card has the same weight, so nothing tells the user what matters most.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/page.tsx:431-676 — every QueueSection renders even when count=0`
- `src/app/page.tsx:227-229 — the Empty component is a single grey sentence`
- `Live: / as bdo at 1024px — two empty cards; / as founder_admin at 375px — four empty cards fill the viewport`

**Recommendation.** When all queues are empty, show one 'You're all caught up' summary with role guidance. For example, BDO: 'KAMs assign you evidence tasks during Stage 2. You'll get a notification and they'll appear here.' Approver: 'Decision rounds open when a case reaches your stage.' Add a single primary CTA where it fits (RM: New Case). When some queues have items, sort non-empty queues first and fold empty ones into a single line ('Nothing in: Drafts · Returned · Approved'). Add a 'next best action' banner showing the single most urgent item across queues.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Code confirms every QueueSection renders at count 0 and that Empty is a single muted sentence (l.227-229). The 'four empty cards fill the viewport' observation is amplified by the empty-data bypass; real users with work will see populated queues. The idle and first-run case is genuine but low impact. It is an opportunity, so downgraded to P3.

*Area:* live-sweep · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam, accounts, founder_admin, ordinary_approver

**Problem.** The header search starts empty on every render, so on /search?q=test the box goes back to its placeholder and refining means retyping. There is no keyboard shortcut, no typeahead, and no quick jump to a case number, which is how RM and KAM users actually look things up. On /cases there are two search boxes, global and list, stacked, which adds to the confusion on mobile.

- `src/components/SearchBar.tsx:10-15 — submit-only, no suggestions`
- `Live: /search?q=test — the header input shows the placeholder 'Search cases or parties...'`

### #148 [P3] Collections screen still uses 27 arbitrary 11px sizes and low-contrast hints
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* accessibility · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* accounts, kam

**Problem.** Item 14 is OPEN. CollectionsClient has 27 text-[11px]/[10px] arbitrary sizes and only 1 use of the text-tiny token. The 11px sizes carry the most important collections signals (DPD pill, PTP date, broken PTP, amount received today, escalation level, bucket labels), and they are hard to read on a busy collections desk and on mobile. The empty-column hint uses text-muted-foreground/60, which likely fails WCAG AA contrast. Using arbitrary sizes also goes against the semantic-token convention.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:814-815 - bucket header labels/counts at 11px`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:896,899,963 - metric labels and hints at 11px`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:967 - 'Drag cases here' at 11px, muted-foreground/60`
- `src/app/collections/CollectionsClient.tsx:987-1010 - DPD, PTP, Broke PTP, received-today, escalation pills at 11px`
- `tailwind.config.js:77 - the text-tiny token exists (10px/14px) but is barely used`

**Recommendation.** Use text-xs (12px) for the decision-bearing pills (DPD, PTP, broken PTP, received today) and keep text-tiny only for secondary hints. Replace /60 opacity text with text-muted-foreground. Add a lint rule (e.g. a no-restricted-syntax regex for text-\[\d+px\]) so arbitrary sizes don't come back.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Count verified: 27 occurrences of text-[11px] and 1 of text-tiny. Correction: there are no text-[10px] occurrences. Line 967 uses text-muted-foreground/60 ('Drag cases here'), which is a real contrast concern. The token convention in memory is mainly about semantic color tokens; arbitrary font sizes are a consistency issue rather than a violation of that rule. 11px versus 12px is a modest readability gain, so P3. Recommendation is reasonable.

### #149 [P3] Settings page shows developer copy for unknown keys and saves live risk controls without a preview
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* copy · *Effort:* S · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** Item 13 is PARTIAL. The tab split (Risk controls / Vocabulary / Identity formats), humanised keys and the 'Credit justification reasons' relabel are done. But any setting not in settingMeta renders 'No description registered — add it to settingMeta.' and 'Blast radius is not yet registered for this control.', which is engineering instruction shown to a business admin. Every value is forced into a numeric input with step 0.1, so a non-numeric setting breaks. Live controls, which carry the banner 'affect live business logic immediately', save with one click and no before/after confirmation, despite the blast-radius text being right there. The component name RcaReasonManager still misnames the vocabulary in code.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:73-74 - fallback description copy aimed at developers`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:82 - 'Blast radius is not yet registered' fallback`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:79 - type=number step=0.1 for every key`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:67,77-80 - live-impact banner, then one-click Save`
- `src/app/settings/page.tsx:9,91 - RcaReasonManager used for 'reason_for_credit'`

**Recommendation.** Hide unregistered keys behind an 'Unregistered (n)' disclosure with neutral copy, or fail a test when a system_settings key has no metadata. Add an input type to settingMeta. Make Save open a confirm that shows 'Change write-off slippage 5% → 8%; 3 cases pending write-off will be re-evaluated'. Rename the component to CreditReasonManager.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Confirmed: the developer fallback copy at :74 and :82 actually appears today. Seeded keys MIN_CLOSED_CASES_FOR_PREAPPROVAL and MIN_STABLE_COVERAGE_PCT_FOR_PREAPPROVAL are not in settingMeta, and the page ignores the system_settings.description column, which holds a business description for them. The better fix is to fall back to setting.description. One-click Save with no confirm is confirmed. Refuted: 'a non-numeric setting breaks', because system_settings.value is numeric NOT NULL and the server parseFloat-validates it. The RcaReasonManager naming is cosmetic.

### #150 [P3] Opportunity: live score impact preview while answering evidence
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* rm, kam, bdo, accounts

**Problem.** Reviewers and RMs answer evidence without seeing what it does to the case. The intake shows only 'Auto-mapped to Grade N', and TaskCompleteForm shows a static weight. They can't tell that one answer moves the case from band B to band A or clears ambiguity, so effort isn't prioritised and surprises turn up at approval.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/cases/new/NewCaseForm.tsx:811-815 - only 'Auto-mapped to Grade N' feedback`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/TaskCompleteForm.tsx:91-93 - static weight line`
- `src/app/cases/[id]/actions.ts:108-126 - per-stage score and band are already computed for the workspace`

**Recommendation.** Once a shared resolveGrade and a pure scoring function exist (see the earlier findings), show a projected line under each answer: 'Stage 2 customer score 62 → 68 · band Silver → Gold (45 → 60 days)'. On the stage card, list the 'highest-impact open tasks' sorted by effective weight × remaining grade headroom, so KAMs chase the evidence that matters first.

*Verification:* partially_confirmed: Valid opportunity. NewCaseForm 811-815 shows only 'Auto-mapped to Grade N', and the weight hint is static. Correction to the evidence: actions.ts:108-126 computes score and band only for past stages. The current stage is explicitly deferred ('current calculation deferred to UI'), so live projection data is not already available. It depends on the pure scoring and resolveGrade refactor from findings 1 and 3. Speculative value, so P3.

### #151 [P3] Opportunity: weight matrix subject filter, capacity totals and bulk edit
*Area:* backlog-status · *Kind:* opportunity · *Effort:* M · *Personas:* founder_admin

**Problem.** The new grid is a big improvement, but it lists customer and contractor parameters together against every persona. Admins have no subject filter, no per-persona column total against stage max, and no bulk action such as copying one persona's overrides to another or resetting a column. Tuning a new persona means dozens of single-cell edits, each a round trip to the server.

**Evidence.**
- `src/app/policy/weights/WeightsClient.tsx:12 - parameters sorted by stage only, no subject split`
- `src/app/policy/weights/WeightsClient.tsx:29 - persona columns with no footer totals`
- `src/app/policy/weights/WeightsClient.tsx:51-59 - one cell saved at a time`

**Recommendation.** Add Customer/Contractor tabs. Add a sticky footer row per stage with each persona's capacity next to stage max_total, and highlight drift. Add column menu actions: 'Copy from persona…', 'Reset to defaults', and 'Scale column by %'. Support keyboard grid editing (Enter to edit, Tab to the next cell, batch save).

*Verification:* confirmed: Verified WeightsClient.tsx: parameters sorted by stage and name with no subject split (:12), no footer totals, and one cell saved per form round trip (:51). Personas are subject-specific (customer_persona_id and contractor_persona_id on the cycle), so contractor parameters shown against customer personas are irrelevant cells. That strengthens the case for a subject filter. Enhancement only.
